[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"status":"fulfilled","value":{"chunk_id":"b765f3","wall_time_seconds":0.2766072,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1877,"output":"export const PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL = \'contact@aspiringattorneys.com\';\\n\\nexport const isPrimaryAdmin = (email: string | null | undefined) =>\\n  (email || \'\').toLowerCase() === PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL;\\n\\nexport interface ManagedUser {\\n  class_id: string;\\n  email: string;\\n  display_name: string | null;\\n  role: string;\\n  last_seen_at: string | null;\\n  /** When the account was made. From auth, not profiles. */\\n  created_at?: string | null;\\n  /** Set when the student has been archived: access off, hidden from the list, records kept. */\\n  archived_at: string | null;\\n  archived_reason: string | null;\\n  /** Which access template the switches match. null = undecided, in the signup queue. */\\n  access_tier: \'tier_1\' | \'tier_2\' | \'tier_3\' | null;\\n  has_bootcamp_access: boolean;\\n  has_classroom_access: boolean;\\n  has_analytics_access: boolean;\\n  has_schedule_access: boolean;\\n  has_practice_access: boolean;\\n  has_drill_access: boolean;\\n  has_waj_access: boolean;\\n  has_flagged_access: boolean;\\n  has_chat_access: boolean;\\n  has_export_access: boolean;\\n  has_ta_access: boolean;\\n  has_journal_access: boolean;\\n  has_activity_access: boolean;\\n}\\n\\nexport interface AdminAnalytics {\\n  total_users: number;\\n  active_today: number;\\n  active_week: number;\\n  admin_count: number;\\n  feature_adoption: Record<string, number>;\\n  total_attempts: number;\\n  week_attempts: number;\\n}\\n\\n// Every door a student can be given, grouped the way the classroom rail groups\\n// them, and labelled in the words the rail uses. This list is the admin UI: a\\n// flag that is missing here has no toggle anywhere, which is how\\n// `has_journal_access` and `has_activity_access` came to be ungrantable while\\n// the rail happily padlocked Wrong answers and Activities against them.\\n//\\n// Keep it in step with `ALL_FLAGS` in supabase/functions/admin-manage-users.\\n// The server writes what it is told; if the client cannot name a flag, no\\n// amount of pressing \\"grant all\\" will turn it on where Joshua can see it.\\nexport const FLAG_GROUPS = [\\n  {\\n    label: \'Practice\',\\n    flags: [\\n      { key: \'has_drill_access\', label: \'Drill\' },\\n      { key: \'has_practice_access\', label: \'Practice tests\' },\\n      { key: \'has_activity_access\', label: \'Activities\' },\\n      { key: \'has_bootcamp_access\', label: \'Bootcamps\' },\\n    ],\\n  },\\n  {\\n    label: \'Review\',\\n    flags: [\\n      { key: \'has_journal_access\', label: \'Wrong answers\' },\\n      { key: \'has_flagged_access\', label: \'Flagged questions\' },\\n      { key: \'has_analytics_access\', label: \'Progress and history\' },\\n      { key: \'has_export_access\', label: \'Export\' },\\n    ],\\n  },\\n  {\\n    label: \'Classroom\',\\n    flags: [\\n      { key: \'has_classroom_access\', label: \'Classroom\' },\\n      { key: \'has_chat_access\', label: \'Messages\' },\\n      { key: \'has_schedule_access\', label: \'Schedule and booking\' },\\n      { key: \'has_ta_access\', label: \'TA worksheets\' },\\n    ],\\n  },\\n] as const;\\n\\nexport const CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS = FLAG_GROUPS.flatMap((group) => group.flags.map((flag) => flag.key));\\n/**\\n * What a paying student gets regardless of which template is applied. Templates\\n * force these back on after the explicit list, so \\"Trial\\" can narrow practice\\n * without taking away the room the student stands in.\\n */\\nexport const DEFAULT_ON_FLAG_KEYS = [\\n  \'has_practice_access\',\\n  \'has_bootcamp_access\',\\n  \'has_classroom_access\',\\n  \'has_analytics_access\',\\n  \'has_schedule_access\',\\n  \'has_waj_access\',\\n  \'has_journal_access\',\\n  \'has_flagged_access\',\\n  \'has_chat_access\',\\n  \'has_export_access\',\\n  \'has_ta_access\',\\n  \'has_activity_access\',\\n] as const;\\n/** Deduped: every configurable flag is now also a default-on candidate. */\\nexport const ALL_FLAG_KEYS = [...new Set<string>([...CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS, ...DEFAULT_ON_FLAG_KEYS])];\\n\\nexport const ALL_FLAG_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {};\\nFLAG_GROUPS.forEach((group) => group.flags.forEach((flag) => {\\n  ALL_FLAG_LABELS[flag.key] = flag.label;\\n}));\\n\\nexport function accessTemplate(enabled: string[]): Record<string, boolean> {\\n  const out: Record<string, boolean> = {};\\n  for (const key of CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS) out[key] = enabled.includes(key);\\n  for (const key of DEFAULT_ON_FLAG_KEYS) out[key] = true;\\n  return out;\\n}\\n\\nexport const ROLE_TEMPLATES: { id: string; label: string; description: string; flags: Record<string, boolean> }[] = [\\n  {\\n    id: \'trial\',\\n    label: \'Trial\',\\n    description: \'Practice and Drill only.\',\\n    flags: accessTemplate([\'has_practice_access\', \'has_drill_access\']),\\n  },\\n  {\\n    id: \'standard\',\\n    label: \'Standard\',\\n    description: \'Core tools plus classroom.\',\\n    flags: accessTemplate([\\n      \'has_practice_access\',\\n      \'has_drill_access\',\\n      \'has_classroom_access\',\\n      \'has_bootcamp_access\',\\n    ]),\\n  },\\n  {\\n    id: \'full\',\\n    label: \'Full\',\\n    description: \'All configurable access.\',\\n    flags: accessTemplate(CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS),\\n  },\\n];\\n\\nexport function templateMatches(user: Record<string, unknown>, template: Record<string, boolean>): boolean {\\n  return CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS.every((key) => Boolean(user[key]) === template[key]);\\n}\\n\\nexport function computeAdminAnalytics(users: ManagedUser[], analytics: AdminAnalytics | null): AdminAnalytics {\\n  return analytics || {\\n    total_users: users.length,\\n    active_today: users.filter((user) => {\\n      if (!user.last_seen_at) return false;\\n      return Date.now() - new Date(user.last_seen_at).getTime() < 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\\n    }).length,\\n    active_week: users.filter((user) => {\\n      if (!user.last_seen_at) return false;\\n      return Date.now() - new Date(user.last_seen_at).getTime() < 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\\n    }).length,\\n    admin_count: users.filter((user) => user.role === \'admin\').length,\\n    feature_adoption: ALL_FLAG_KEYS.reduce((acc, key) => {\\n      acc[key] = users.filter((user) => user[key as keyof ManagedUser] === true).length;\\n      return acc;\\n    }, {} as Record<string, number>),\\n    total_attempts: 0,\\n    week_attempts: 0,\\n  };\\n}\\n\\n// ── Access tiers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n// A tier is a template: it sets the twelve switches above, and the switches\\n// stay what the app checks. The tier column only names the template the\\n// switches currently match; the database relabels Tier 1 or Tier 3 as Tier 2\\n// the moment a switch moves away from its template.\\n\\nexport type AccessTier = \'tier_1\' | \'tier_2\' | \'tier_3\';\\n\\n/** Classroom home, Messages, Schedule: what every account keeps. */\\nexport const FLOOR_FLAG_KEYS = [\'has_classroom_access\', \'has_chat_access\', \'has_schedule_access\'] as const;\\n\\nexport const ACCESS_TIERS: { id: AccessTier; label: string; description: string }[] = [\\n  { id: \'tier_1\', label: \'Tier 1\', description: \'Everything on.\' },\\n  { id: \'tier_2\', label: \'Tier 2\', description: \'You choose each feature.\' },\\n  { id: \'tier_3\', label: \'Tier 3\', description: \'Classroom, Messages and Schedule, plus what you assign.\' },\\n];\\n\\nexport const TIER_LABELS: Record<AccessTier, string> = {\\n  tier_1: \'Tier 1\',\\n  tier_2: \'Tier 2\',\\n  tier_3: \'Tier 3\',\\n};\\n\\n/** The switches a tier writes. Tier 2 writes nothing: it keeps what is there. */\\nexport function tierFlags(tier: AccessTier): Record<string, boolean> | null {\\n  if (tier === \'tier_2\') return null;\\n  const out: Record<string, boolean> = {};\\n  for (const key of CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS) {\\n    out[key] = tier === \'tier_1\' || (FLOOR_FLAG_KEYS as readonly string[]).includes(key);\\n  }\\n  return out;\\n}\\n"}}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"status":"fulfilled","value":{"chunk_id":"c43ef5","wall_time_seconds":0.254024,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":3393,"output":"import { type ReactNode } from \\"react\\";\\nimport { useLocation, useParams, useSearchParams } from \\"react-router-dom\\";\\nimport { useQuery } from \\"@tanstack/react-query\\";\\nimport { useAuth } from \\"@/contexts/AuthContext\\";\\nimport { useUserPermissions, type PermissionFlag } from \\"@/hooks/useUserPermissions\\";\\nimport { supabase } from \\"@/integrations/supabase/client\\";\\nimport ProtectedRoute from \\"@/components/ProtectedRoute\\";\\nimport { loadPracticeTestRun } from \\"@/lib/practiceTest\\";\\nimport {\\n  launchFromRouteState,\\n  parseDrillLaunch,\\n  type DrillHandoverState,\\n  type DrillLaunch,\\n  type PtLegRef,\\n} from \\"@/lib/drillLaunch\\";\\n\\n/**\\n * Per item, never per feature.\\n *\\n * A student on Tier 3 has the floor and whatever Joshua assigns. Assigning one\\n * activity must open that activity, not the Activity Centre; assigning one\\n * PrepTest must open that sitting, not the Testing Centre. So an assignment\\n * authorises its own runner: this gate lets the child render when the feature\\n * switch is on OR when `authorize` proves the signed-in student owns the\\n * specific thing in the route. Otherwise it falls through to the ordinary\\n * ProtectedRoute, which shows the locked state.\\n *\\n * Same shape as BootcampGate, which has done this for bootcamps all along.\\n * Route-level only: RLS already scopes every assignment table by student_id,\\n * so this decides what a student is shown, not what they can read.\\n */\\nexport function AssignmentGate({\\n  flag,\\n  authKey,\\n  authorize,\\n  children,\\n}: {\\n  flag: PermissionFlag | readonly PermissionFlag[];\\n  /** Stable name for the thing being authorised, for caching. null = nothing to authorise. */\\n  authKey: string | null;\\n  authorize: (userId: string) => Promise<boolean>;\\n  children: ReactNode;\\n}) {\\n  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();\\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\\n  const flags = (Array.isArray(flag) ? flag : [flag]) as PermissionFlag[];\\n  const flagOpen = permissions.is_admin || flags.some((f) => Boolean(permissions[f]));\\n\\n  const { data: assigned, isLoading } = useQuery<boolean>({\\n    queryKey: [\\"assignment-gate\\", user?.id, authKey],\\n    enabled: authReady && !!user && !permissions.loading && !flagOpen && !!authKey,\\n    queryFn: () => authorize(user!.id),\\n    staleTime: 60_000,\\n  });\\n\\n  const waiting =\\n    authReady && !!user && !permissions.loading && !flagOpen && !!authKey && isLoading;\\n  if (waiting) {\\n    return (\\n      <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center\\">\\n        <div className=\\"w-6 h-6 border-2 border-muted border-t-foreground rounded-full animate-spin\\" />\\n      </div>\\n    );\\n  }\\n\\n  if (!flagOpen && assigned) return <>{children}</>;\\n  return <ProtectedRoute flag={flag}>{children}</ProtectedRoute>;\\n}\\n\\n// ── What each runner accepts as proof ──────────────────────────────────────\\n\\nasync function ownsRow(table: string, id: string, userId: string): Promise<boolean> {\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\n    .from(table as never)\\n    .select(\\"id\\")\\n    .eq(\\"id\\", id)\\n    .eq(\\"student_id\\", userId)\\n    .maybeSingle();\\n  if (error) return false;\\n  return !!data;\\n}\\n\\n/** A live practice-test assignment for exactly this PrepTest. */\\nexport async function hasPracticeTestAssignment(userId: string, pt?: number): Promise<boolean> {\\n  let query = supabase\\n    .from(\\"practice_test_assignments\\" as never)\\n    .select(\\"id\\")\\n    .eq(\\"student_id\\", userId)\\n    .neq(\\"status\\", \\"archived\\");\\n  if (typeof pt === \\"number\\") query = query.eq(\\"pt\\", pt);\\n  const { data, error } = await query.limit(1);\\n  if (error) return false;\\n  return (data ?? []).length > 0;\\n}\\n\\nasync function hasAssignedBootcamp(userId: string): Promise<boolean> {\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\n    .from(\\"classroom_items\\")\\n    .select(\\"id\\")\\n    .eq(\\"student_id\\", userId)\\n    .eq(\\"type\\", \\"bootcamp_module\\")\\n    .neq(\\"status\\", \\"archived\\")\\n    .limit(1);\\n  if (error) return false;\\n  return (data ?? []).length > 0;\\n}\\n\\n/** The PrepTest a sitting leg belongs to, read from the run on this device. */\\nfunction ptOfRun(runId: string | null | undefined): number | null {\\n  if (!runId) return null;\\n  return loadPracticeTestRun(runId)?.pt ?? null;\\n}\\n\\n// ── Route wrappers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\n/** /activities/:assignmentId */\\nexport function ActivityAssignmentGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\\n  const { assignmentId } = useParams<{ assignmentId: string }>();\\n  return (\\n    <AssignmentGate\\n      flag=\\"has_activity_access\\"\\n      authKey={assignmentId ? `activity:${assignmentId}` : null}\\n      authorize={(uid) => ownsRow(\\"activity_assignments\\", assignmentId!, uid)}\\n    >\\n      {children}\\n    </AssignmentGate>\\n  );\\n}\\n\\n/** /classroom/ta/:assignmentId */\\nexport function TAAssignmentGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\\n  const { assignmentId } = useParams<{ assignmentId: string }>();\\n  return (\\n    <AssignmentGate\\n      flag=\\"has_ta_access\\"\\n      authKey={assignmentId ? `ta:${assignmentId}` : null}\\n      authorize={(uid) => ownsRow(\\"ta_assignments\\", assignmentId!, uid)}\\n    >\\n      {children}\\n    </AssignmentGate>\\n  );\\n}\\n\\n/** /testing: the Testing Centre lists only assigned PrepTests without the switch. */\\nexport function TestingCentreGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\\n  return (\\n    <AssignmentGate\\n      flag=\\"has_practice_access\\"\\n      authKey=\\"testing:any\\"\\n      authorize={(uid) => hasPracticeTestAssignment(uid)}\\n    >\\n      {children}\\n    </AssignmentGate>\\n  );\\n}\\n\\n/** /testing/test/:runId */\\nexport function PracticeTestRunGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\\n  const { runId } = useParams<{ runId: string }>();\\n  return (\\n    <AssignmentGate\\n      flag=\\"has_practice_access\\"\\n      authKey={runId ? `practice-run:${runId}` : null}\\n      authorize={async (uid) => {\\n        if (!runId) return false;\\n        const { data, error } = await supabase\\n          .from(\\"practice_test_runs\\" as never)\\n          .select(\\"pt,status\\")\\n          .eq(\\"id\\", runId)\\n          .eq(\\"user_id\\", uid)\\n          .maybeSingle();\\n        if (!error && data) {\\n          const accountRun = data as { pt: number; status: string };\\n          return accountRun.status === \\"complete\\" ||\\n            hasPracticeTestAssignment(uid, accountRun.pt);\\n        }\\n        const localPt = ptOfRun(runId);\\n        return localPt !== null && hasPracticeTestAssignment(uid, localPt);\\n      }}\\n    >\\n      {children}\\n    </AssignmentGate>\\n  );\\n}\\n\\n/**\\n * What an assignment-launched drill run is authorised by, if anything. Pure so\\n * it can be tested without a router.\\n */\\nexport type DrillAuthorisation =\\n  | { kind: \\"homework\\"; id: string }\\n  | { kind: \\"guided\\"; id: string }\\n  | { kind: \\"practice-test\\"; pt: number }\\n  | { kind: \\"bootcamp\\" }\\n  | null;\\n\\nexport function drillAuthorisation(\\n  launch: DrillLaunch | null,\\n  ptRunId: string | null,\\n  ptOf: (runId: null,\\n): DrillAuthorisation {\\n  if (ptRunId) {\\n    const pt = ptOf(ptRunId);\\n    if (pt !== null) return { kind: \\"practice-test\\", pt };\\n  }\\n  if (!launch) return legacyAssignmentId ? { kind: \\"homework\\", id: legacyAssignmentId } : null;\\n  if (launch.kind === \\"questions\\" && launch.carry) {\\n    const carry = launch.carry;\\n    if (carry.ptLeg?.runId) {\\n      const pt =   if (carry.assignmentId) return { kind: \\"homework\\", id: carry.assignmentId };\\n  }\\n  switch (launch.kind) {\\n    case \\"assignment\\":\\n      return { kind: \\"homework\\", id: launch.assignmentId };\\n    case \\"guided-drill\\":\\n      return launch.assignmentId ? { kind: \\"guided\\", id: launch.assignmentId } : null;\\n    case \\"bootcamp-day\\":\\n    case \\"practice-set\\":\\n      return { kind: \\"bootcamp\\" };\\n    case \\"section\\":\\n      if (launch.ptLeg?.runId) {\\n        const pt = ptOf(launch.ptLeg.runId);\\n        if (pt !== null) return { kind: \\"practice-test\\", pt };\\n      }\\n      return null;\\n    default:\\n      return null;\\n  }\\n}\\n\\nasync function authoriseDrill(auth: NonNullable<DrillAuthorisation>, uid: ownsRow(\\"guided_drill_assignments\\", auth.id, uid);\\n    case \\"practice-test\\":\\n      return hasPracticeTestAssignment(uid, auth.pt);\\n    case \\"bootcamp\\":\\n      return hasAssignedBootcamp(uid);\\n  }\\n}\\n\\n/** A sitting grants its active LR section only, never a fresh ad-hoc drill. */\\nexport function isActivePracticeTestLrLeg(\\n  row: { pt: number; status: string; current_leg: number; legs: Array<{ kind: string; section: number; startedAt?: string; completedAt?: string }> },\\n  launch: DrillLaunch | null,\\n  runId: string,\\n): boolean {\\n  if (row.status !== \'in-progress\' || launch?.kind !== \'section\') return false;\\n  const leg = row.legs?.[row.current_leg];\\n  return !!leg && leg.kind === \'lr\' && !!leg.startedAt && !leg.completedAt &&\\n    launch.ptLeg?.runId === runId && launch.ptLeg.legIndex === row.current_leg &&\\n    launch.pt === row.pt && launch.section === leg.section;\\n}\\n\\n/** Resuming RC requires completedAt?: string; passageIds?: number[] }> },\\n  session: { user_id: string; practice_test_run_id: string | null; practice_test_leg: number | null; status: string; passage_ids: number[] },\\n  userId: string,\\n  runId: string,\\n  legIndex: number,\\n): boolean {\\n  const leg = row.legs?.[row.current_leg];\\n  return row.status === \'in-progress\' && leg?.kind === \'rc\' &&   session.status === \'in_progress\' &&\\n    JSON.stringify([...session.passage_ids].sort((a, b) => a - b)) ===\\n      JSON.stringify([...(leg.passageIds ?? [])].sort((a, b) => a - b));\\n}\\n\\nfunction authKeyOf(auth: DrillAuthorisation): string | null {\\n  if (!auth) return null;\\n  switch (auth.kind) {\\n    case \\"homework\\":\\n    case \\"guided\\":\\n      return `${auth.kind}:${auth.id}`;\\n    case \\"practice-test\\":\\n      return `pt:${auth.pt}`;\\n    case \\"bootcamp\\":\\n      return \\"bootcamp:any\\";\\n  }\\n}\\n\\n/**\\n * /drill, /rc and /rc/drill. The runner is shared, so the Drill switch opens it\\n * for anything, and an assignment opens it for that one run: a homework set, an\\n * assigned guided drill, an assigned bootcamp\'s days, or a leg of an assigned\\n * PrepTest sitting.\\n */\\nexport function DrillAssignmentGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\\n  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();\\n  const location = useLocation();\\n  const state = location.state as (DrillHandoverState & { ptRun?: PtLegRef }) | null;\\n\\n  let launch: DrillLaunch | null = null;\\n  try {\\n    launch = parseDrillLaunch(searchParams) ?? launchFromRouteState(state, state?.ptRun);\\n  } catch {\\n    launch = null;\\n  }\\n  const ptRunId = state?.ptRun?.runId ?? searchParams.get(\\"ptRun\\") ??\\n    (launch?.kind === \'section\' ? launch.ptLeg?.runId : null) ??\\n    (launch?.kind === \'questions\' ? launch.carry?.ptLeg?.runId : null);\\n  const auth = drillAuthorisation(launch, ptRunId, undefined, searchParams.get(\\"assignmentId\\"));\\n  const rcSessionId = location.pathname === \'/rc/drill\' ? searchParams.get(\'sessionId\') : null;\\n  const rcLegParam = searchParams.get(\'ptLeg\');\\n  const rcLegIndex = rcLegParam !== null && /^\\\\d+$/.test(rcLegParam) ? Number(rcLegParam) : null;\\n\\n  return (\\n    <AssignmentGate\\n      flag=\\"has_drill_access\\"\\n      authKey={ptRunId ? `practice-leg:${ptRunId}:${launch?.kind === \'section\' ? ptRunId)\\n            .eq(\'practice_test_leg\', rcLegIndex)\\n            .maybeSingle();\\n          if (sessionError || !session || !isActivePracticeTestRcLeg(run, session, uid, ptRunId, ## assistant

Verified against local `origin/main`, which contains the tier code matching the screenshot. The current working checkout is an older, heavily modified branch; I made no edits.

- **Tier 1:** all 12 visible switches enabled.
- **Tier 2:** no preset at all. Selecting it preserves every current switch. Consequently, switching Tier 1 → Tier 2 leaves everything enabled.
- **Tier 3:** Classroom, Messages, Schedule enabled; Drill, Practice tests, Activities, Bootcamps, Wrong answers, Flagged questions, Progress/history, Export, TA worksheets disabled.
- The hidden legacy `has_waj_access` is set false when applying Tier 1 or Tier 3; the visible Wrong answers flag remains the relevant consolidated control.
- Database trigger relabels Tier 1 or Tier 3 as Tier 2 when switches deviate from their template.
- Assigned work can open its particular runner even with its category switch off.

**TA worksheets means Teaching Assistant assignments.** Joshua approves a draft from the Teaching Assistant; that creates a student-specific `ta_assignments` row with title, HTML content, optional assets/PDF, and potentially structured response fields/answer key. Students can view, fill in, and complete these assignments. They are separate from the Activities catalogue and ordinary question drills.

**The TA worksheet switch is effectively redundant for students’ own assignments:** `/classroom/ta/:assignmentId` opens if the flag is on **or** the signed-in student owns that particular assignment. RLS already restricts students to their own rows. Turning the switch on does not reveal a general worksheet catalogue; turning it off does not block assigned worksheets.

**No fixed worksheet inventory or count exists in this model.** Worksheets are created per assignment, including by approving Teaching Assistant drafts. A live database count would measure existing assignment rows, not the number of worksheet products students receive. I have not queried the live count.

Relevant references in `origin/main`:

- `src/lib/adminUsers.ts:180–214`: definitions and tier flags.
- `src/lib/accessTierClient.ts:35–61`: apply tier, including fallback write.
- `src/components/AssignmentGate.tsx:126`: TA assignment gate.
- `src/components/ta/DraftCard.tsx:37–61`: approved drafts create assignments.
- `src/hooks/useStudentAssignments.ts:140–202`: assignment shape and student filtering.
- `supabase/migrations/20260923010100_access_tiers_stage2_tier_column.sql:97`: automatic relabelling.

Prior decisions:

- **2026-09-22:** Tier 1 all-on; Tier 2 custom switch configuration; Tier 3 assignment-only plus Classroom/Messages/Schedule. No access expiry, payment automation, prior Tier 2 snapshots, pricing effects, or student-facing tier labels.
- **2026-09-12:** discussed complete suspension and access duration. The completed 2026-09-22 interview later rejected automatic access changes and defined the Tier 3 floor. Avoid reintroducing duration as a tier default.
- The user’s current request replaces the earlier “Tier 2 is custom” design with a meaningful middle preset. That is the primary interview branch.

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.4 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"de3d3b","wall_time_seconds":0.2648134,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":2647,"output":"import { useEffect } from \\"react\\";\\nimport { useQuery, useQueryClient } from \\"@tanstack/react-query\\";\\nimport { useLocation, useNavigate } from \\"react-router-dom\\";\\nimport { supabase } from \\"@/integrations/supabase/client\\";\\nimport { useAuth } from \\"@/contexts/AuthContext\\";\\nimport { authUrlWithReturnTo } from \\"@/lib/authRedirect\\";\\n\\nexport interface UserPermissions {\\n  has_bootcamp_access: boolean;\\n  has_classroom_access: boolean;\\n  has_analytics_access: boolean;\\n  has_schedule_access: boolean;\\n  has_practice_access: boolean;\\n  has_drill_access: boolean;\\n  has_waj_access: boolean;\\n  has_journal_access: boolean;\\n  has_flagged_access: boolean;\\n  has_chat_access: boolean;\\n  has_export_access: boolean;\\n  has_ta_access: boolean;\\n  has_activity_access: boolean;\\n  is_admin: boolean;\\n  loading: boolean;\\n}\\n\\nconst ACCESS_FLAGS = [\\n  \\"has_bootcamp_access\\",\\n  \\"has_classroom_access\\",\\n  \\"has_analytics_access\\",\\n  \\"has_schedule_access\\",\\n  \\"has_practice_access\\",\\n  \\"has_drill_access\\",\\n  \\"has_waj_access\\",\\n  \\"has_journal_access\\",\\n  \\"has_flagged_access\\",\\n  \\"has_chat_access\\",\\n  \\"has_export_access\\",\\n  \\"has_ta_access\\",\\n  \\"has_activity_access\\",\\n] as const;\\n\\nexport type PermissionFlag = (typeof ACCESS_FLAGS)[number];\\n\\n// Narrow session-death classifier. Only three conditions count:\\n//  1. supabase.auth.getSession() returns no session while context says user is set\\n//  2. Supabase query error with HTTP status 401\\n//  3. Error message mentioning JWT / token-expired / invalid_token explicitly\\n// Everything else (including RLS 403s and null profile rows) is legitimate\\n// permission-denied and must fall through to the normal LockedModule path.\\nfunction isSessionDeadError(err: any): boolean {\\n  if (!err) return false;\\n  if (err.status === 401) return true;\\n  const msg = String(err.message || \\"\\").toLowerCase();\\n  return (\\n    msg.includes(\\"jwt\\") ||\\n    msg.includes(\\"token is expired\\") ||\\n    msg.includes(\\"token expired\\") ||\\n    msg.includes(\\"invalid_token\\")\\n  );\\n}\\n\\ntype PermissionFlags = Omit<UserPermissions, \\"loading\\">;\\n\\nconst DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS: PermissionFlags = {\\n  has_bootcamp_access: false,\\n  has_classroom_access: false,\\n  has_analytics_access: false,\\n  has_schedule_access: false,\\n  has_practice_access: false,\\n  has_drill_access: false,\\n  has_waj_access: false,\\n  has_journal_access: false,\\n  has_flagged_access: false,\\n  has_chat_access: false,\\n  has_export_access: false,\\n  has_ta_access: false,\\n  has_activity_access: false,\\n  is_admin: false,\\n};\\n\\n// Sentinel thrown by the query fn when the session is dead. The hook reacts to\\n// it once (sign out + /auth bounce) instead of navigating from inside the\\n// queryFn, which may run detached from a render.\\nclass SessionExpiredError extends Error {}\\n\\n// Thrown when the signed-in student\'s profile is stamped archived. An archived\\n// account keeps every record, but it may not be inside the app: the session is\\n// ended immediately rather than left running with all doors shut.\\nclass AccountArchivedError extends Error {}\\n\\n// One realtime channel per signed-in user, shared by every mounted consumer.\\n// An UPDATE to the student\'s own profiles row (an admin flipping a flag)\\n// invalidates the cached permissions, so a revoke takes effect on the next\\n// render rather than when the stale window runs out. RLS scopes the stream to\\n// the student\'s own row; profiles is in the supabase_realtime publication\\n// (migration 20260922120000).\\ntype WatchListener = (row: Record<string, unknown> | null) => void;\\ntype ProfileWatch = { channel: ReturnType<typeof supabase.channel>; listeners: Set<WatchListener> };\\nconst profileWatches = new Map<string, ProfileWatch>();\\n\\nfunction acquireProfileWatch(userId: string, onChange: WatchListener): WatchListener {\\n  let watch = profileWatches.get(userId);\\n  if (!watch) {\\n    const listeners = new Set<WatchListener>();\\n    const channel = supabase\\n      .channel(`profile-access-${userId}`)\\n      .on(\\n        \\"postgres_changes\\",\\n        { event: \\"UPDATE\\", schema: \\"public\\", table: \\"profiles\\", filter: `class_id=eq.${userId}` },\\n        (payload) => {\\n          const row = (payload?.new ?? null) as Record<string, unknown> | null;\\n          listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(row));\\n        },\\n      )\\n      .subscribe();\\n    watch = { channel, listeners };\\n    profileWatches.set(userId, watch);\\n  }\\n  watch.listeners.add(onChange);\\n  return onChange;\\n}\\n\\nfunction releaseProfileWatch(userId: string, listener: WatchListener) {\\n  const watch = profileWatches.get(userId);\\n  if (!watch) return;\\n  watch.listeners.delete(listener);\\n  if (watch.listeners.size === 0) {\\n    profileWatches.delete(userId);\\n    void supabase.removeChannel(watch.channel);\\n  }\\n}\\n\\nexport function useUserPermissions(): UserPermissions {\\n  const { user, authReady, signOut } = useAuth();\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\n  const location = useLocation();\\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\\n  const userId = user?.id;\\n\\n  // The hook mounts once per ProtectedRoute, so the subscription is guarded by\\n  // a single shared channel per user: the first mount opens it, the last unmount\\n  // closes it. Without this, every gated page would hold its own socket.\\n  useEffect(() => {\\n    if (!authReady || !userId) return;\\n    const entry = acquireProfileWatch(userId, (row) => {\\n      // Ignore self-caused / unrelated row updates (e.g. presence heartbeats\\n      // writing last_seen_at). Only an actual access-flag change needs a\\n      // refetch; otherwise every heartbeat would re-trigger the permissions\\n      // query, which used to feed a fetch -> write -> event -> fetch loop.\\n      if (row) {\\n        const cached = queryClient.getQueryData<PermissionFlags>([\\"user-permissions\\", userId]);\\n        if (cached) {\\n          const archivedNow = \\"archived_at\\" in row && !!row.archived_at;\\n          const changed = ACCESS_FLAGS.some(\\n            (flag) => flag in row && !!row[flag] !== !!cached[flag],\\n          );\\n          if (!changed && !archivedNow) return;\\n        }\\n      }\\n      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\\"user-permissions\\", userId] });\\n    });\\n    return () => releaseProfileWatch(userId, entry);\\n  }, [authReady, userId, queryClient]);\\n\\n  const query = useQuery<PermissionFlags>({\\n    // Cached per user, shared across all ProtectedRoute / consumer mounts, so\\n    // navigating between gated pages reads cached permissions synchronously\\n    // instead of re-fetching (and re-showing the full-screen loading gate).\\n    queryKey: [\\"user-permissions\\", userId],\\n    enabled: authReady && !!user,\\n    // Realtime (below) is the primary path for a revoke to land; the short\\n    // stale window and the focus refetch are the fallback when the socket is\\n    // down, so \\"Revoke All Access\\" is never more than a tab switch away from\\n    // taking effect. Cached data keeps `loading` false, so neither flashes.\\n    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,\\n    gcTime: 30 * 60_000,\\n    refetchOnWindowFocus: true,\\n    // Retry transient failures a couple times, but never retry a dead session\\n    // (that path signs the user out).\\n    retry: (count, err) =>\\n      !(err instanceof SessionExpiredError) && !(err instanceof AccountArchivedError) && count < 2,\\n    queryFn: async () => {\\n      const userId = user!.id;\\n\\n      // Pre-check: is the session still alive on the client?\\n      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();\\n      if (!session) throw new SessionExpiredError();\\n\\n      // Fetch access flags from profile\\n      const { data: profile, error: profileErr } = await supabase\\n        .from(\\"profiles\\")\\n        .select([...ACCESS_FLAGS, \\"archived_at\\"].join(\\", \\"))\\n        .eq(\\"class_id\\", userId)\\n        .maybeSingle();\\n      if (isSessionDeadError(profileErr)) throw new SessionExpiredError();\\n      // A non-session read failure (transient network / 5xx) must NOT be\\n      // silently converted to all-false permissions; throw so TanStack Query\\n      // records the error and retries instead of caching bogus denied flags.\\n      if (profileErr) throw profileErr;\\n\\n      // Fetch admin role\\n      const { data: roleData, error: roleErr } = await supabase\\n        .from(\\"user_roles\\")\\n        .select(\\"role\\")\\n        .eq(\\"user_id\\", userId)\\n        .eq(\\"role\\", \\"admin\\")\\n        .maybeSingle();\\n      if (isSessionDeadError(roleErr)) throw new SessionExpiredError();\\n      if (roleErr) throw roleErr;\\n\\n      const profileRow = (profile ?? null) as unknown as Record<string, unknown> | null;\\n      // Archived: no flags, no app. Sign them out on the spot.\\n      if (profileRow?.archived_at) throw new AccountArchivedError();\\n\\n      const flagValues = { ...DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS };\\n      for (const flag of ACCESS_FLAGS) {\\n        flagValues[flag] = Boolean(profileRow?.[flag]);\\n      }\\n      flagValues.is_admin = !!roleData;\\n\\n      // Presence is owned by usePresenceHeartbeat; this read stays read-only.\\n      // Writing last_seen_at here produced a write per permissions fetch and\\n      // (with profiles in the realtime publication) a self-sustaining\\n      // fetch -> write -> event -> fetch loop.\\n\\n\\n\\n      return flagValues;\\n    },\\n  });\\n\\n  // A dead session bounces to /auth once. Preserve lsatu_saved_email so\\n  // re-login is smooth after an expiry bounce, and carry where they were: a\\n  // bare \\"/auth\\" discarded the pathname, query, and hash, so a student whose\\n  // token expired on an assignment link lost the link as well as the session.\\n  useEffect(() => {\\n    if (query.error instanceof AccountArchivedError) {\\n      (async () => {\\n        await signOut({ keepSavedEmail: true });\\n        navigate(\\"/auth\\", { replace: true, state: { reason: \\"account_archived\\" } });\\n      })();\\n    } else if (query.error instanceof SessionExpiredError) {\\n      const returnTo = `${location.pathname}${location.search}${location.hash}`;\\n      (async () => {\\n        await signOut({ keepSavedEmail: true });\\n        navigate(authUrlWithReturnTo(returnTo), {\\n          replace: true,\\n          state: { reason: \\"session_expired\\" },\\n        });\\n      })();\\n    } else if (query.error) {\\n      console.error(\\"Failed to fetch permissions\\", query.error);\\n    }\\n    // `location` is intentionally not a dependency: this must capture where the\\n    // user was when the session died, not follow them as they navigate.\\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\\n  }, [query.error, signOut, navigate]);\\n\\n  return {\\n    ...DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS,\\n    ...(query.data ?? {}),\\n    // Only the true cold load (no cached data) blocks; cached reads and\\n    // background refetches keep loading false, so gated navigations don\'t flash.\\n    loading: authReady && !!user && query.isPending,\\n  };\\n}\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"81d8c2","wall_time_seconds":0.2688446,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1049,"output":"import { type ReactNode } from \\"react\\";\\nimport { Navigate, useLocation } from \\"react-router-dom\\";\\nimport { useAuth } from \\"@/contexts/AuthContext\\";\\nimport { useUserPermissions, type PermissionFlag } from \\"@/hooks/useUserPermissions\\";\\nimport { authUrlWithReturnTo } from \\"@/lib/authRedirect\\";\\nimport LockedModule from \\"@/components/LockedModule\\";\\n\\ninterface ProtectedRouteProps {\\n  /**\\n   * Optional feature flag, or a list of acceptable feature flags. Ignored when\\n   * `requireAdmin` is true.\\n   */\\n  flag?: PermissionFlag | readonly PermissionFlag[];\\n  /**\\n   * Allow any signed-in user through even when the feature flag is false.\\n   * Useful for core student workspace pages that should stay reachable while\\n   * their contents are still constrained by row-level data access.\\n   *\\n   * \\"Signed in is enough\\" — never \\"nobody needs to be signed in\\". A route that\\n   * should be public is not wrapped in this component at all.\\n   */\\n  allowSignedIn?: boolean;\\n  /**\\n   * When true, only admin users may render the children. Non-admin\\n   * users, regardless of feature flags, see LockedModule.\\n   */\\n  requireAdmin?: boolean;\\n  children: ReactNode;\\n}\\n\\nconst LOCAL_DEV_HOSTS = new Set([\\"localhost\\", \\"127.0.0.1\\", \\"::1\\"]);\\n\\n/**\\n * Opt-in only. A plain `npm run dev` now exercises the real permission path, so\\n * a gating regression is visible locally instead of surfacing after deploy.\\n * Set VITE_DEV_BYPASS_GATES=1 in .env.local to walk gated routes without the\\n * matching flags.\\n *\\n * It relaxes *flags*, never authentication — the caller applies it only after\\n * the signed-in check, so no configuration can make this component render to an\\n * anonymous visitor.\\n */\\nfunction isLocalDevAccessAllowed() {\\n  return (\\n    import.meta.env.DEV &&\\n    import.meta.env.VITE_DEV_BYPASS_GATES === \\"1\\" &&\\n    LOCAL_DEV_HOSTS.has(window.location.hostname)\\n  );\\n}\\n\\nexport default function ProtectedRoute({ flag, requireAdmin, allowSignedIn, children }: ProtectedRouteProps) {\\n  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();\\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\\n  const location = useLocation();\\n\\n  // Auth first, and nothing before it. `useUserPermissions` reports\\n  // `loading: authReady && !!user && isPending`, which is *false* while auth is\\n  // still resolving — so a check on `!user` alone read \\"not yet known\\" as\\n  // \\"signed out\\" and bounced every cold deep link to /auth. Holding here until\\n  // auth is definitively resolved is what makes an assignment link survive.\\n  if (!authReady) {\\n    return (\\n      <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center\\">\\n        <div className=\\"w-6 h-6 border-2 border-muted border-t-foreground rounded-full animate-spin\\" />\\n      </div>\\n    );\\n  }\\n\\n  // Authentication is intrinsic to this component: every shape of it requires a\\n  // user. The check used to run only for `requireAdmin` or flagged routes, so a\\n  // wrapper with `allowSignedIn` and no flag fell through to the unconditional\\n  // render at the bottom and served protected content to anonymous visitors.\\n  if (!user) {\\n    const returnTo = `${location.pathname}${location.search}${location.hash}`;\\n    return <Navigate to={authUrlWithReturnTo(returnTo)} replace />;\\n  }\\n\\n  // After the auth checks, never before: the dev bypass opens locked features\\n  // on a local machine, not the front door.\\n  if (isLocalDevAccessAllowed()) {\\n    return <>{children}</>;\\n  }\\n\\n  // Only a true cold read blocks; cached reads and background refetches keep\\n  // this false, so gated navigations do not flash.\\n  if (permissions.loading) {\\n    return (\\n      <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center\\">\\n        <div className=\\"w-6 h-6 border-2 border-muted border-t-foreground rounded-full animate-spin\\" />\\n      </div>\\n    );\\n  }\\n\\n  if (requireAdmin) {\\n    return permissions.is_admin ? <>{children}</> : <LockedModule />;\\n  }\\n\\n  if (allowSignedIn) {\\n    return <>{children}</>;\\n  }\\n\\n  const flags = Array.isArray(flag) ? flag : flag ? [flag] : [];\\n  if (flags.length > 0) {\\n    if (permissions.is_admin || flags.some((f) => permissions[f])) {\\n      return <>{children}</>;\\n    }\\n    return <LockedModule />;\\n  }\\n\\n  return <>{children}</>;\\n}\\n"}'}]

'{"chunk_id":"c275ce","wall_time_seconds":0.2934248,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":686,"output":"import DOMPurify from \\"dompurify\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Generate and trigger download of a PDF for a TA assignment.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Client-side path: sanitizes `contentHtml`, renders it into an offscreen\\r\\n * container using the page\'s existing prose styles, rasterizes via\\r\\n * html2canvas, and packs into a multi-page A4 PDF via jsPDF.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Both libs are lazy-imported so they only hit the bundle when a student\\r\\n * actually clicks Download.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function downloadAssignmentPdf(params: {\\r\\n  title: string;\\r\\n  contentHtml: string;\\r\\n}): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const { title, contentHtml } = params;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const [{ default: jsPDF }, html2canvasMod] = await Promise.all([\\r\\n    import(\\"jspdf\\"),\\r\\n    import(\\"html2canvas\\"),\\r\\n  ]);\\r\\n  const html2canvas = html2canvasMod.default;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const sanitized = DOMPurify.sanitize(contentHtml || \\"\\");\\r\\n\\r\\n  const container = document.createElement(\\"div\\");\\r\\n  container.style.position = \\"fixed\\";\\r\\n  container.style.left = \\"-10000px\\";\\r\\n  container.style.top = \\"0\\";\\r\\n  container.style.width = \\"780px\\";\\r\\n  container.style.padding = \\"32px\\";\\r\\n  container.style.background = \\"#ffffff\\";\\r\\n  container.style.color = \\"#111111\\";\\r\\n  container.style.fontFamily =\\r\\n    \'system-ui, -apple-system, \\"Segoe UI\\", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif\';\\r\\n  container.style.fontSize = \\"14px\\";\\r\\n  container.style.lineHeight = \\"1.55\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n  const heading = document.createElement(\\"h1\\");\\r\\n  heading.textContent = title || \\"Assignment\\";\\r\\n  heading.style.fontSize = \\"22px\\";\\r\\n  heading.style.fontWeight = \\"600\\";\\r\\n  heading.style.marginBottom = \\"16px\\";\\r\\n  container.appendChild(heading);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const body = document.createElement(\\"div\\");\\r\\n  body.innerHTML = sanitized;\\r\\n  container.appendChild(body);\\r\\n\\r\\n  document.body.appendChild(container);\\r\\n\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    const canvas = await html2canvas(container, {\\r\\n      backgroundColor: \\"#ffffff\\",\\r\\n      scale: 2,\\r\\n      useCORS: true,\\r\\n    });\\r\\n\\r\\n    const imgData = canvas.toDataURL(\\"image/png\\");\\r\\n    const pdf = new jsPDF({ unit: \\"pt\\", format: \\"a4\\" });\\r\\n    const pageWidth = pdf.internal.pageSize.getWidth();\\r\\n    const pageHeight = pdf.internal.pageSize.getHeight();\\r\\n\\r\\n    const imgWidth = pageWidth;\\r\\n    const imgHeight = (canvas.height * imgWidth) / canvas.width;\\r\\n\\r\\n    let heightLeft = imgHeight;\\r\\n    let position = 0;\\r\\n\\r\\n    pdf.addImage(imgData, \\"PNG\\", 0, position, imgWidth, imgHeight);\\r\\n    heightLeft -= pageHeight;\\r\\n\\r\\n    while (heightLeft > 0) {\\r\\n      position -= pageHeight;\\r\\n      pdf.addPage();\\r\\n      pdf.addImage(imgData, \\"PNG\\", 0, position, imgWidth, imgHeight);\\r\\n      heightLeft -= pageHeight;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    const safeTitle =\\r\\n      (title || \\"assignment\\").replace(/[^a-z0-9-_]+/gi, \\"_\\").slice(0, 64) ||\\r\\n      \\"assignment\\";\\r\\n    pdf.save(`${safeTitle}.pdf`);\\r\\n  } finally {\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

Confirmed: `has_export_access` is an inert switch in `origin/main`. No route or export action consumes it.

Existing exports:

- **TA assignment detail:** Download PDF exports assignment title and HTML content as an A4 PDF. Available upon accessing that assignment, including through assignment ownership with TA switch off. `ClassroomRouteViews.tsx:650,723`; `taPdf.ts:13`.
- **Principle-Flaw bootcamp Review Log:** Export CSV downloads all saved log rows with Date, Ref, Type, Confidence, Picked, Correct, Takeaway. No Export permission check. `ReviewLog.tsx:140,176`.
- **Teacher canvas:** Markdown and PDF exports. Teacher surface controls availability; Export switch does not. `DeskCanvasPanel.tsx:109`; `canvasExports.ts:99`.

Wrong answers and Flagged questions require their **own independent switches**. Drill or Practice tests does not implicitly grant either. `App.tsx:629–630,669–674`; `useUserPermissions.ts` returns profile flags directly.

Assignment ownership opens particular runners but does not open Wrong answers or Flagged lists. Related gap: assigned Activities/timed sections can run with feature switch off, yet their review detail routes still require Activity/Drill switches (`App.tsx:624–625`). The review queue also omits Activity, RC, section and bootcamp sources unless their feature flags are on. Completed practice-test review loads for any signed-in student (`ReviewQueuePage.tsx:47–54`). This distinction matters if Tier 3 is expected to review everything assigned.

## assistant

“Progress and history” gives the student access to three screens:

- **Overview** (`/progress/overview`): LR and RC first-answer accuracy over a chosen date window; charts of question-type/difficulty performance, including repeat attempts; suggested improvement areas; imported metrics; a flagged-question list.
- **History** (`/progress/history`): their chronological practice records, per-question attempts/results/time, completed timed sections, and saved first/repeat answer history.
- **Topics** (`/progress/topics`): accuracy by LR question type, counting each question’s first saved graded answer once. No comparable RC topic breakdown currently exists.

Disabling it locks those screens and the Library lesson’s “Your saved evidence” panel. It **does not stop saving results**, delete history, block assigned work, or independently disable Wrong answers/Flagged questions. Those review lists have separate flags.

A coupling worth surfacing: Analytics itself renders a flagged-question list without consulting `has_flagged_access`, so turning Flagged off does not hide every appearance of flags if Progress remains enabled. Analytics also offers buttons that launch drills, but those launches still encounter Drill’s own gate.

Library Lessons accepts **any** of Activity, Bootcamp, Drill, or Analytics access. Disabling Progress alone does not remove the lesson when another qualifying switch stays on.

References: `App.tsx:635–642`; `Analytics.tsx:96–104,347–354`; `DrillHistory.tsx:1–14`; `ProgressTopics.tsx:24–27`; `StudentLibraryLessons.tsx:53,88`.

Freshness: verified against cached `origin/main` commit **67230df**, dated **2026-10-01 19:27:19 America/Toronto**. I have not fetched or inspected the live deployment, so this describes that local main snapshot, not a verified live rendering.

It sets the twelve switches; the switches stay what the\\n-- app checks. access_tier only names which template the switches currently\\n-- match, so the People page can say \\"Tier 1\\" without re-deriving it.\\n--\\n--   NULL    undecided: sits in the signup queue, floor access only\\n--   tier_1  all twelve switches on\\n--   tier_2  hand-picked switches\\n--   tier_3  the floor (Classroom, Messages, Schedule) plus assigned work\\n\\nALTER TABLE public.profiles\\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS access_tier text;\\n\\nALTER TABLE public.profiles\\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS profiles_access_tier_check;\\nALTER TABLE public.profiles\\n  ADD CONSTRAINT profiles_access_tier_check\\n  CHECK (access_tier IS NULL OR access_tier IN (\'tier_1\', \'tier_2\', \'tier_3\'));\\n\\nCOMMENT ON COLUMN public.profiles.access_tier IS\\n  \'Access template the switches match. NULL = undecided (signup queue). Label only: the has_*_access switches are what the app enforces.\';\\n\\n-- The tier is an admin act, like the switches it names.\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_profile_admin_fields()\\n RETURNS trigger\\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\\n SET search_path TO \'public\'\\nAS $function$\\nBEGIN\\n  IF auth.uid() IS NULL OR public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\') THEN\\n    RETURN NEW;\\n  END IF;\\n\\n  IF NEW.has_bootcamp_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_bootcamp_access  OR\\n     NEW.has_classroom_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_classroom_access OR\\n     NEW.has_analytics_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_analytics_access OR\\n     NEW.has_schedule_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_schedule_access  OR\\n     NEW.has_practice_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_practice_access  OR\\n     NEW.has_drill_access     IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_drill_access     OR\\n     NEW.has_waj_access       IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_waj_access       OR\\n     NEW.has_journal_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_journal_access   OR\\n     NEW.has_flagged_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_flagged_access   OR\\n     NEW.has_chat_access      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_chat_access      OR\\n     NEW.has_export_access    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_export_access    OR\\n     NEW.has_ta_access        IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_ta_access        OR\\n     NEW.has_activity_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_activity_access  OR\\n     NEW.access_tier          IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.access_tier          OR\\n     NEW.archived_at          IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_at          OR\\n     NEW.archived_reason      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_reason      OR\\n     NEW.proficiency_level    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.proficiency_level    OR\\n     NEW.class_id             IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.class_id             THEN\\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'profiles: access flags, access tier, archive state, proficiency_level and class_id are admin-controlled\';\\n  END IF;\\n\\n  RETURN NEW;\\nEND;\\n$function$;\\n\\n-- Drift: a Tier 1 or Tier 3 label only holds while the switches match it.\\n-- Flip one switch away from the template and the label becomes Tier 2. Label\\n-- only, never access: the switch that was flipped is the change, this just\\n-- stops the page lying about it. Lives in the database so every write path,\\n-- including a bulk update, keeps the label honest.\\n--\\n-- A write that sets access_tier itself (set_tier, archive, reset) is trusted\\n-- and left alone, which is what lets set_tier move a row between templates.\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.profiles_access_tier_drift()\\n RETURNS trigger\\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\\n SET search_path TO \'public\'\\nAS $function$\\nDECLARE\\n  all_on boolean;\\n  floor_only boolean;\\nBEGIN\\n  IF NEW.access_tier IS NULL OR NEW.access_tier = \'tier_2\' THEN\\n    RETURN NEW;\\n  END IF;\\n  IF TG_OP = \'UPDATE\' AND NEW.access_tier IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.access_tier THEN\\n    RETURN NEW;\\n  END IF;\\n\\n  all_on :=\\n    NEW.has_drill_access AND NEW.has_practice_access AND NEW.has_activity_access AND\\n    NEW.has_bootcamp_access AND NEW.has_journal_access AND NEW.has_flagged_access AND\\n    NEW.has_analytics_access AND NEW.has_export_access AND NEW.has_classroom_access AND\\n    NEW.has_chat_access AND NEW.has_schedule_access AND NEW.has_ta_access;\\n\\n  floor_only :=\\n    NEW.has_classroom_access AND NEW.has_chat_access AND NEW.has_schedule_access AND NOT (\\n      NEW.has_drill_access OR NEW.has_practice_access OR NEW.has_activity_access OR\\n      NEW.has_bootcamp_access OR NEW.has_journal_access OR NEW.has_flagged_access OR\\n      NEW.has_analytics_access OR NEW.has_export_access OR NEW.has_ta_access\\n    );\\n\\n  IF NEW.access_tier = \'tier_1\' AND NOT all_on THEN\\n    NEW.access_tier := \'tier_2\';\\n  ELSIF NEW.access_tier = \'tier_3\' AND NOT floor_only THEN\\n    NEW.access_tier := \'tier_2\';\\n  END IF;\\n\\n  RETURN NEW;\\nEND;\\n$function$;\\n\\nDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS profiles_access_tier_drift_trg ON public.profiles;\\nCREATE TRIGGER profiles_access_tier_drift_trg\\n  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.profiles\\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.profiles_access_tier_drift();\\n"}}'}]

A tier is a template: Tier 1 turns all twelve switches\\r\\n      // on, Tier 3 leaves the floor (Classroom, Messages, Schedule) and turns\\r\\n      // the other nine off, Tier 2 keeps whatever switches are there and only\\r\\n      // changes the label. One update, so the student never sits half-applied.\\r\\n      if (action === \\"set_tier\\") {\\r\\n        const { class_id, tier } = body;\\r\\n        if (!class_id || !TIERS.includes(tier)) {\\r\\n          return jsonRes({ error: \\"Invalid params\\" }, 400);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        const updates: Record<string, unknown> = { access_tier: tier };\\r\\n        if (tier !== \\"tier_2\\") {\\r\\n          for (const flag of TIER_FLAGS) {\\r\\n            updates[flag] = tier === \\"tier_1\\" || FLOOR_FLAGS.includes(flag);\\r\\n          }\\r\\n          updates.has_waj_access = false;\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        const { data: updated, error } = await admin\\r\\n          .from(\\"profiles\\")\\r\\n          .update(updates)\\r\\n          .eq(\\"class_id\\", class_id)\\r\\n          .select(\\"class_id, access_tier, \\" + ALL_FLAGS.join(\\", \\"));\\r\\n        if (error) throw error;\\r\\n        if (!updated || updated.length === 0) {\\r\\n          console.error(\\"set_tier: no profile matched\\", { class_id, tier });\\r\\n          return jsonRes({ error: \\"No profile matched that class_id\\" }, 404);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: updated[0] });\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Assign one PrepTest sitting. Opens that sitting and nothing else, and\\r\\n      // puts it on the student\'s Assigned work like any other assignment.\\r\\n      if (action === \\"assign_practice_test\\") {\\r\\n        const { class_id, pt, unlock_at = null } = body;\\r\\n        const ptNum = Number(pt);\\r\\n        if (!class_id || !Number.isInteger(ptNum) || ptNum <= 0) {\\r\\n          return jsonRes({ error: \\"Invalid params\\" }, 400);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        const { data: assignment, error } = await admin\\r\\n          .from(\\"practice_test_assignments\\")\\r\\n          .upsert(\\r\\n            {\\r\\n              student_id: class_id,\\r\\n              pt: ptNum,\\r\\n              assigned_by: callerId,\\r\\n              assigned_at: new Date().toISOString(),\\r\\n              unlock_at,\\r\\n"}'}]

Never report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This is the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions — a hard gate, not a suggestion.\\r\\n- **Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** Narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A pushed \\"fix\\" that does not hold costs a second full session; this has already happened once.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Workflow Expectations\\r\\n\\r\\n- Run `npm run build` before wrapping up significant code or documentation work.\\r\\n- If tests are relevant to the change, run them too.\\r\\n- After completing a change, stage the intended files, commit with a concise message, and push to `origin main` unless the user says otherwise.\\r\\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session, so a raw push can be rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges `origin/main` if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes. It also gates on `npm run typecheck`.\\r\\n- **Never roll `main` back.** Do not use Lovable\'s \\"Revert to commit\\", \\"revert and resend\\", or history restore, and never reset or force-push `main`. On 2026-09-27 an accidental \\"revert and resend\\" (b998596) silently undid three merged PRs across 121 question-bank files, and students saw the wrong questions again. To undo your own work, `git revert <your SHA>` only. If a Lovable dialog offers a revert, dismiss it; if one fires, stop and tell Joshua before repairing anything.\\r\\n- Existing Codex repo hooks live in `.codex/hooks.json`; preserve them unless the task explicitly requires a hook change.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Session Protocol (second brain)\\r\\n\\r\\nProduct context, status, and decisions live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`, not this repo. Two repo-local skills in `.agents/skills/` run the session ritual; the canonical protocol is in the vault `CLAUDE.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe vault was rebuilt 2026-07-16 (paths relative to `~/ai-os`). Live: teaching voice `wiki/teaching-voice.md` (route ALL student-facing copy through it), the business `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`, a student\'s record `students/<name>/` (see `students/README.md`), teaching knowledge `corpus/index.md`, and the `wiki/{tasks,hot,dashboard,index,log}.md` hubs named below. Archived read-only reference (pre-rebuild product knowledge, may be stale): `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/` — product context `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md`, design system `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`, quality bar `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`, domain index `…/index.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n- `pickup` — start of session. Read `wiki/tasks.md` (the Now / Next / Done ledger) first, then `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, skim `wiki/index.md`, read the latest `wiki/log.md` entry. Emit a 6-8 line briefing (where we left off, Now, Next, open threads, hot, suggested next) and wait. Do not deep-read other pages until pointed at a task.\\r\\n- `wrapup` — end of session. Propose which `## Now` tasks finished and confirm before writing; a wrong \\"Done\\" is the failure to avoid. Then strike finished items through in `wiki/tasks.md` with a date, refresh `wiki/hot.md`, append a dated `wiki/log.md` entry (newest first, distinct label for same-day sessions), update the task anchor.\\r\\n\\r\\n`wiki/tasks.md` is the single source of truth for what is open vs done. Finished work is struck through so a new session never re-runs it. `raw/` in the vault is immutable.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Codex Adapter Layout\\r\\n\\r\\n- `.codex/config.toml`: minimal shared Codex project config. Keep secrets and machine-specific overrides out of this file.\\r\\n- `.codex/agents/`: Codex agent definitions converted from `.claude/agents/*.md` when Claude agents exist. Use `.toml` files with converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.\\r\\n- `.agents/skills/`: repository-local Codex skills copied from `.claude/skills/` when Claude skills exist and Codex should use them. Keep each `SKILL.md` with its supporting files.\\r\\n- `.codex/hooks.json`: existing repository hooks. Preserve unless the task explicitly concerns hooks.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis checkout currently has Claude settings and hooks under `.claude/`, but no `.claude/skills/` or `.claude/agents/` directory to convert.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Known Gaps\\r\\n\\r\\n- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.\\r\\n- No Prettier config and no CI workflows.\\r\\n- `README.md` is still minimal.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Agent skills\\r\\n\\r\\n### Issue tracker\\r\\n\\r\\nGitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Triage labels\\r\\n\\r\\nThe five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Domain docs\\r\\n\\r\\nMulti-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. See `docs/agents/domain.md`.\\r\\n"}'}]

Prior Tier 2 was custom switches, not a settled middle preset. This request reopens that default.\\r\\n- Prior decisions retained provisionally: manual tier selection; no automatic payment grants or expiry; assigned work authorizes its own runner; per-student overrides persist.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Q&A log\\r\\n### Starting request: fixed defaults\\r\\n- Captured: “Tier one should have all enabled, tier two should have certain ones enabled, and tier three should have even less.”\\r\\n- Requested investigation: explain “TA worksheets,” including how many if applicable.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Open flags (pending input)\\r\\n- Current preset and TA worksheet implementation research -> Jay 2.\\r\\n- Tier 2 and Tier 3 intended student use and exact switches -> Joshua.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Q1 — Tier 3 default\\r\\n- Captured: Tier 3 enables only Classroom, Messages, Schedule and booking, and Export.\\r\\n- All other feature switches default off.\\r\\n- This adds Export to the earlier three-feature floor.\\r\\n- Assigned-work runner behavior remains a prior decision, not re-confirmed by this answer.\\r\\n- Follow-up requested: explain what a TA worksheet is before deciding further.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Research — TA worksheets and current presets\\r\\n- Verified in local origin/main source, not a fresh production database check: TA means Teaching Assistant assignments. Approving a Teaching Assistant draft creates a student-specific worksheet with HTML content, optional assets/PDF, and sometimes response fields/answer key.\\r\\n- No fixed worksheet catalogue/count. Each worksheet is an assignment created for a student.\\r\\n- TA switch is redundant for assigned worksheets: AssignmentGate allows the owning student to open their assigned TA worksheet even when the switch is off. It does not grant a browseable catalogue.\\r\\n- Current Tier 2 applies no switch changes. Switching from Tier 1 to Tier 2 therefore retains everything on.\\r\\n- Current Tier 3 excludes Export, so Joshua’s new preset requires a change when implementation is authorized after discovery.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Q2 — Remove redundant switches and bundle review\\r\\n- Captured: remove TA worksheets from the tier/access controls. Context interpreted as removing its toggle; deletion of assignment content is not authorized.\\r\\n- Drill includes Wrong answers and Flagged questions.\\r\\n- Practice tests also includes Wrong answers and Flagged questions.\\r\\n- Remove independent Wrong answers and Flagged questions toggles.\\r\\n- Derived access rule: either Drill OR Practice tests enables both review features.\\r\\n- Joshua asks what Export does; source investigation pending.\\r\\n- Tier 2’s full preset remains undecided.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Research — Export\\r\\n- Verified against local origin/main source: has_export_access has no runtime consumer gating a route or export action. It is stored and displayed, but currently changes no export behavior.\\r\\n- Existing download/export actions (including assigned TA PDF download and Principle-Flaw review CSV) do not check it.\\r\\n- Recommendation pending Joshua’s answer: remove this ineffective toggle and let downloads remain part of the feature the student can access.\\r\\n- Implementation has not started; tier discovery continues.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Q3 — Remove Export toggle\\r\\n- Asked: remove the ineffective Export toggle and include downloads within accessible features?\\r\\n- Captured: Joshua confirmed “yes.”\\r\\n- Remove Export as an independent access control; downloads belong to the feature the student can access.\\r\\n- Updated Tier 3 visible preset: Classroom, Messages, Schedule and booking only.\\r\\n- Supersedes the earlier request to include Export as a Tier 3 switch.\\r\\n- Remaining visible controls: Drill, Practice tests, Activities, Bootcamps, Progress and history, Classroom, Messages, Schedule and booking.\\r\\n- Tier 2 default remains pending.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Q4 — Pause to explain Progress and history\\r\\n- Asked: approve proposed Tier 2 defaults?\\r\\n- Captured: Joshua is confused by “Progress and history” and requests an explanation before continuing.\\r\\n- Tier 2 preset is not approved. “everyone drilling practice tests” is ambiguous and not treated as a settled default.\\r\\n- Interview questions paused while Jay 2 checks what this control actually gates.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Research — Progress and history\\r\\n- Source-verified in local origin/main: has_analytics_access gates Progress Overview, History and Topics.\\r\\n- Overview summarizes practice performance, including accuracy by question type/difficulty and first answers versus repeat attempts.\\r\\n- History shows past practice and question attempts, including results and time.\\r\\n- Topics summarizes Logical Reasoning topic accuracy.\\r\\n- Turning this access off locks those pages; it does not stop saving practice results or independently control Wrong answers/Flagged access.\\r\\n- Explanation is source-based, not a fresh production UI verification.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Q5 — Bundle Progress and history\\r\\n- Captured: remove the independent Progress and history toggle.\\r\\n- Automatically enable its pages when Activities OR Practice tests OR Drill is enabled.\\r\\n- Joshua explicitly included Activities in this rule.\\r\\n- Wrong answers and Flagged questions remain bundled with Drill OR Practice tests, per Q2; extending those to Activities has not been decided.\\r\\n- Remaining visible switches: Drill, Practice tests, Activities, Bootcamps, Classroom, Messages, Schedule and booking.\\r\\n- Tier 1: all seven enabled.\\r\\n- Tier 3: Classroom, Messages, Schedule and booking enabled; four practice/content switches off.\\r\\n- Tier 2: pending exact selection.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Q6 — Correct Progress and history dependency\\r\\n- Captured: Joshua explicitly excludes Activities from enabling Progress and history.\\r\\n- Corrected rule: Drill OR Practice tests automatically enables Progress and history.\\r\\n- Supersedes Q5’s Activities OR Practice tests OR Drill rule.\\r\\n- Unified review dependency: Drill OR Practice tests includes Wrong answers, Flagged questions, and Progress and history, with no independent toggles for those three.\\r\\n- Tier 2 proposed preset remains unanswered.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Q7 — Activities are assignment-only\\r\\n- Captured: Activities should be accessible only when Joshua assigns them to the student from the admin side.\\r\\n- Remove Activities as a tier/access toggle.\\r\\n- The specific assignment authorizes access to that activity; no general Activities catalogue access through a tier.\\r\\n- Applies across all tiers, including Tier 1. Tier 1 “everything enabled” now means all remaining tier-controlled features, not unassigned Activities.\\r\\n- Remaining visible switches: Drill, Practice tests, Bootcamps, Classroom, Messages, Schedule and booking.\\r\\n- Activities do not enable Progress and history.\\r\\n- Tier 2 and Bootcamp access policy remain pending.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Q8 — Bootcamps are assignment-only\\r\\n- Asked: should Bootcamps follow Activities, assigned access with no toggle?\\r\\n- Captured: Joshua confirmed “yes it is the same for bootcamp.”\\r\\n- Remove Bootcamps from tier switches. An admin assignment grants access to that specific bootcamp across every tier.\\r\\n- Remaining tier-controlled switches: Drill, Practice tests, Classroom, Messages, Schedule and booking.\\r\\n- Drill OR Practice tests automatically includes Wrong answers, Flagged questions, Progress and history.\\r\\n- Activities and Bootcamps remain assignment-only, irrespective of tier.\\r\\n- Open decision: what separates Tier 2 from Tier 1 now that Activities and Bootcamps are no longer tier-controlled?\\r\\n\\r\\n### Q9 — Tier 2 clarification requested\\r\\n- Joshua’s message contained both “add the drill for Tier 2” and an explicit statement that Tier 2 should have only Classroom, messaging, schedule and booking, with no Drill or tests.\\r\\n- Working interpretation from the explicit feature list: Tier 2 has Classroom, Messages, Schedule and booking; Drill and Practice tests off. The contradictory opening was surfaced for clarification.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Q10 — Tier 3 reduced floor\\r\\n- Captured: “Tier 3 should only give access to schedule and booking and message.”\\r\\n- Confirmed Tier 3: Messages and Schedule and booking enabled; Classroom, Drill and Practice tests disabled.\\r\\n- Supersedes earlier Tier 3 Classroom floor.\\r\\n- Proposed final distinction: Tier 1 all five; Tier 2 Classroom/Messages/Schedule and booking; Tier 3 Messages/Schedule and booking.\\r\\n- Need confirmation of Tier 2 interpretation before implementation because Q9’s conflicting Drill statement remains unresolved explicitly.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Q11 — Tier 2 confirmed, assigned runners preserved\\r\\n- Captured: Tier 2 should not have independent Drill or Practice test access.\\r\\n- Joshua can explicitly assign specific drills or sections through Classroom, and Tier 2 students can open and complete those specific assignments.\\r\\n- Assignment access must not unlock the general Drill or Practice tests feature.\\r\\n- Final presets: Tier 1 all five switches; Tier 2 Classroom/Messages/Schedule and booking; Tier 3 Messages/Schedule and booking.\\r\\n- Implement assigned drill/section authorization separately from general practice permission.\\r\\n- Discovery of tier defaults is complete. Implementation requested (“Let’s fix it”) and pending.\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"status":"fulfilled","value":{"chunk_id":"9777af","wall_time_seconds":0.373461,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":3293,"output":"export const PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL = \'contact@aspiringattorneys.com\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const isPrimaryAdmin = (email: string | null | undefined) =>\\r\\n  (email || \'\').toLowerCase() === PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface ManagedUser {\\r\\n  class_id: string;\\r\\n  email: string;\\r\\n  display_name: string | null;\\r\\n  role: string;\\r\\n  last_seen_at: string | null;\\r\\n  /** When the account was made. From auth, not profiles. */\\r\\n  created_at?: string | null;\\r\\n  /** Set when the student has been archived: access off, hidden from the list, records kept. */\\r\\n  archived_at: string | null;\\r\\n  archived_reason: string | null;\\r\\n  /** Which access template the switches match. null = undecided, in the signup queue. */\\r\\n  access_tier: \'tier_1\' | \'tier_2\' | \'tier_3\' | null;\\r\\n  has_bootcamp_access: boolean;\\r\\n  has_classroom_access: boolean;\\r\\n  has_analytics_access: boolean;\\r\\n  has_schedule_access: boolean;\\r\\n  has_practice_access: boolean;\\r\\n  has_drill_access: boolean;\\r\\n  has_waj_access: boolean;\\r\\n  has_flagged_access: boolean;\\r\\n  has_chat_access: boolean;\\r\\n  has_export_access: boolean;\\r\\n  has_ta_access: boolean;\\r\\n  has_journal_access: boolean;\\r\\n  has_activity_access: boolean;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface AdminAnalytics {\\r\\n  total_users: number;\\r\\n  active_today: number;\\r\\n  active_week: number;\\r\\n  admin_count: number;\\r\\n  feature_adoption: Record<string, number>;\\r\\n  total_attempts: number;\\r\\n  week_attempts: number;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// Every door a student can be given, grouped the way the classroom rail groups\\r\\n// them, and labelled in the words the rail uses. This list is the admin UI: a\\r\\n// flag that is missing here has no toggle anywhere, which is how\\r\\n// `has_journal_access` and `has_activity_access` came to be ungrantable while\\r\\n// the rail happily padlocked Wrong answers and Activities against them.\\r\\n//\\r\\n// Keep it in step with `ALL_FLAGS` in supabase/functions/admin-manage-users.\\r\\n// The server writes what it is told; if the client cannot name a flag, no\\r\\n// amount of pressing \\"grant all\\" will turn it on where Joshua can see it.\\r\\nexport const FLAG_GROUPS = [\\r\\n  {\\r\\n    label: \'Practice\',\\r\\n    flags: [\\r\\n      { key: \'has_drill_access\', label: \'Drill\' },\\r\\n      { key: \'has_practice_access\', label: \'Practice tests\' },\\r\\n      { key: \'has_activity_access\', label: \'Activities\' },\\r\\n      { key: \'has_bootcamp_access\', label: \'Bootcamps\' },\\r\\n    ],\\r\\n  },\\r\\n  {\\r\\n    label: \'Review\',\\r\\n    flags: [\\r\\n      { key: \'has_journal_access\', label: \'Wrong answers\' },\\r\\n      { key: \'has_flagged_access\', label: \'Flagged questions\' },\\r\\n      { key: \'has_analytics_access\', label: \'Progress and history\' },\\r\\n      { key: \'has_export_access\', label: \'Export\' },\\r\\n    ],\\r\\n  },\\r\\n  {\\r\\n    label: \'Classroom\',\\r\\n    flags: [\\r\\n      { key: \'has_classroom_access\', label: \'Classroom\' },\\r\\n      { key: \'has_chat_access\', label: \'Messages\' },\\r\\n      { key: \'has_schedule_access\', label: \'Schedule and booking\' },\\r\\n      { key: \'has_ta_access\', label: \'TA worksheets\' },\\r\\n    ],\\r\\n  },\\r\\n] as const;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS = FLAG_GROUPS.flatMap((group) => group.flags.map((flag) => flag.key));\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * What a paying student gets regardless of which template is applied. Templates\\r\\n * force these back on after the explicit list, so \\"Trial\\" can narrow practice\\r\\n * without taking away the room the student stands in.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport const DEFAULT_ON_FLAG_KEYS = [\\r\\n  \'has_practice_access\',\\r\\n  \'has_bootcamp_access\',\\r\\n  \'has_classroom_access\',\\r\\n  \'has_analytics_access\',\\r\\n  \'has_schedule_access\',\\r\\n  \'has_waj_access\',\\r\\n  \'has_journal_access\',\\r\\n  \'has_flagged_access\',\\r\\n  \'has_chat_access\',\\r\\n  \'has_export_access\',\\r\\n  \'has_ta_access\',\\r\\n  \'has_activity_access\',\\r\\n] as const;\\r\\n/** Deduped: every configurable flag is now also a default-on candidate. */\\r\\nexport const ALL_FLAG_KEYS = [...new Set<string>([...CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS, ...DEFAULT_ON_FLAG_KEYS])];\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const ALL_FLAG_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {};\\r\\nFLAG_GROUPS.forEach((group) => group.flags.forEach((flag) => {\\r\\n  ALL_FLAG_LABELS[flag.key] = flag.label;\\r\\n}));\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function accessTemplate(enabled: string[]): Record<string, boolean> {\\r\\n  const out: Record<string, boolean> = {};\\r\\n  for (const key of CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS) out[key] = enabled.includes(key);\\r\\n  for (const key of DEFAULT_ON_FLAG_KEYS) out[key] = true;\\r\\n  return out;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const ROLE_TEMPLATES: { id: string; label: string; description: string; flags: Record<string, boolean> }[] = [\\r\\n  {\\r\\n    id: \'trial\',\\r\\n    label: \'Trial\',\\r\\n    description: \'Practice and Drill only.\',\\r\\n    flags: accessTemplate([\'has_practice_access\', \'has_drill_access\']),\\r\\n  },\\r\\n  {\\r\\n    id: \'standard\',\\r\\n    label: \'Standard\',\\r\\n    description: \'Core tools plus classroom.\',\\r\\n    flags: accessTemplate([\\r\\n      \'has_practice_access\',\\r\\n      \'has_drill_access\',\\r\\n      \'has_classroom_access\',\\r\\n      \'has_bootcamp_access\',\\r\\n    ]),\\r\\n  },\\r\\n  {\\r\\n    id: \'full\',\\r\\n    label: \'Full\',\\r\\n    description: \'All configurable access.\',\\r\\n    flags: accessTemplate(CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS),\\r\\n  },\\r\\n];\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function templateMatches(user: Record<string, unknown>, template: Record<string, boolean>): boolean {\\r\\n  return CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS.every((key) => Boolean(user[key]) === template[key]);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function computeAdminAnalytics(users: ManagedUser[], analytics: AdminAnalytics | null): AdminAnalytics {\\r\\n  return analytics || {\\r\\n    total_users: users.length,\\r\\n    active_today: users.filter((user) => {\\r\\n      if (!user.last_seen_at) return false;\\r\\n      return Date.now() - new Date(user.last_seen_at).getTime() < 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\\r\\n    }).length,\\r\\n    active_week: users.filter((user) => {\\r\\n      if (!user.last_seen_at) return false;\\r\\n      return Date.now() - new Date(user.last_seen_at).getTime() < 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\\r\\n    }).length,\\r\\n    admin_count: users.filter((user) => user.role === \'admin\').length,\\r\\n    feature_adoption: ALL_FLAG_KEYS.reduce((acc, key) => {\\r\\n      acc[key] = users.filter((user) => user[key as keyof ManagedUser] === true).length;\\r\\n      return acc;\\r\\n    }, {} as Record<string, number>),\\r\\n    total_attempts: 0,\\r\\n    week_attempts: 0,\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// ── Access tiers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\r\\n// A tier is a template: it sets the twelve switches above, and the switches\\r\\n// stay what the app checks. The tier column only names the template the\\r\\n// switches currently match; the database relabels Tier 1 or Tier 3 as Tier 2\\r\\n// the moment a switch moves away from its template.\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type AccessTier = \'tier_1\' | \'tier_2\' | \'tier_3\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Classroom home, Messages, Schedule: what every account keeps. */\\r\\nexport const FLOOR_FLAG_KEYS = [\'has_classroom_access\', \'has_chat_access\', \'has_schedule_access\'] as const;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const ACCESS_TIERS: { id: AccessTier; label: string; description: string }[] = [\\r\\n  { id: \'tier_1\', label: \'Tier 1\', description: \'Everything on.\' },\\r\\n  { id: \'tier_2\', label: \'Tier 2\', description: \'You choose each feature.\' },\\r\\n  { id: \'tier_3\', label: \'Tier 3\', description: \'Classroom, Messages and Schedule, plus what you assign.\' },\\r\\n];\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const TIER_LABELS: Record<AccessTier, string> = {\\r\\n  tier_1: \'Tier 1\',\\r\\n  tier_2: \'Tier 2\',\\r\\n  tier_3: \'Tier 3\',\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The switches a tier writes. Tier 2 writes nothing: it keeps what is there. */\\r\\nexport function tierFlags(tier: AccessTier): Record<string, boolean> | null {\\r\\n  if (tier === \'tier_2\') return null;\\r\\n  const out: Record<string, boolean> = {};\\r\\n  for (const key of CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS) {\\r\\n    out[key] = tier === \'tier_1\' || (FLOOR_FLAG_KEYS as readonly string[]).includes(key);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return out;\\r\\n}\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport { tierFlags, type AccessTier, type ManagedUser } from \'@/lib/adminUsers\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Tier reads and writes that work whether or not the new admin-manage-users\\r\\n * function is deployed. The frontend can ship ahead of the function (edge\\r\\n * functions deploy through Lovable, separately from a publish), and an older\\r\\n * function neither returns access_tier nor knows set_tier or the practice-test\\r\\n * actions. Admins can read and write profiles directly under RLS, so each path\\r\\n * falls back to the table when the function cannot do the job.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Without the read fallback the People page would show every student as\\r\\n * \\"No tier\\", and the signup queue would offer to put all of them on Tier 3.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Fill access_tier from profiles when the list came from an older function. */\\r\\nexport async function withAccessTiers(users: ManagedUser[]): Promise<ManagedUser[]> {\\r\\n  if (users.length === 0 || users.some((u) => \'access_tier\' in u)) return users;\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\'profiles\')\\r\\n    .select(\'class_id, access_tier\' as never);\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  const tiers = new Map(\\r\\n    ((data ??       </header>\\r\\n\\r\\n        <section className=\\"mt-8\\" aria-labelledby=\\"conditional-reasoning-title\\">\\r\\n          <button\\r\\n            type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n
