</span>\\r\\n            <h2 id=\\"conditional-reasoning-title\\" className=\\"ac-display mt-2 text-2xl text-foreground\\">Conditional Reasoning</h2>\\r\\n            <p className=\\"mt-1.5 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n              General Conditionals, Linking, Missing Link, and Must Be True.\\r\\n            </p>\\r\\n            <span className=\\"mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-foreground\\">\\r\\n              Choose a drill <ArrowRight className=\\"h-4 w-4\\" aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n            </span>\\r\\n          </button>\\r\\n        </section>\\r\\n\\r\\n        <div className=\\"mt-8\\">\\r\\n          {loading && (\\r\\n            <div className=\\"flex justify-center py-12\\">\\r\\n              <div className=\\"h-6 w-6 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-[hsl(var(--muted))] border-t-[hsl(var(--ac-emerald))]\\" />\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n          )}\\r\\n\\r\\n          {!loading && error && (\\r\\n            <p className=\\"rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card p-5 text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n              Could not load your activities: {error}\\r\\n person.display_name?.toLowerCase().includes(q);\\r\\n      // Archived people are out of the way everywhere except their own\\r\\n      // filter. That is the point of archiving: the list should show who you\\r\\n      // are actually teaching.\\r\\n      const isArchived = !!person.archived_at;\\r\\n      if (roleFilter === \'archived\') return matchesSearch && isArchived;\\r\\n      if (isArchived) return false;\\r\\n\\r\\n      const matchesRole =\\r\\n        roleFilter === \'all\' ||\\r\\n        (roleFilter === \'students\' && person.role !== \'admin\') ||\\r\\n        (roleFilter === \'admins\' && person.role === \'admin\') ||\\r\\n        (roleFilter === \'no-drill\' && person.role !== \'admin\' && !person.has_drill_access);\\r\\n      return matchesSearch && matchesRole;\\r\\n    });\\r\\n  }, [people, roleFilter, search]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const toggleAccess = async (classId: string, field: string, value: boolean) => {\\r\\n    setUsers((prev) => prev.map((user) => (user.class_id === classId ? { ...user, [field]: value } : user)));\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      const res = await supabase.functions.invoke(\'admin-manage-users\', {\\r\\n        method: \'POST\',\\r\\n        body: { class_id: classId, field, value },\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      if (res.error) throw res.error;\\r\\n      // Trust the row the server wrote back rather than the optimistic guess,\\r\\n      // so a toggle that never landed can\'t look like it worked.\\r\\n      const written = (res.data as { profile?: Record<string, unknown> } | null)?.profile;\\r\\n      if (!written || written[field] !== value) throw new Error(\'Permission was not saved\');\\r\\n      // One switch off a Tier 1 or Tier 3 template relabels the student Tier 2\\r\\n      // in the database. Re-read so the label on screen matches.\\r\\n      void fetchData();\\r\\n    } catch (error) {\\r\\n      setUsers((prev) => prev.map((user) => (user.class_id === classId ? { ...user, [field]: !value } : user)));\\r\\n      console.error(\'toggleAccess failed\', { classId, field, value, error });\\r\\n      toast.error(\'Failed to update permission\');\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Apply a tier. Revoking turns every flag off, classroom\\r\\n  // included: the /classroom route lets any signed-in user land, so the\\r\\n  // student still has somewhere to stand, but the home page shows them an\\r\\n  // \\"access paused\\" notice instead of their coursework. Leaving classroom on\\r\\n  // used to keep assigned work, notes and recaps open after a revoke.\\r\\n  // Granting opens everything back up.\\r\\n  const setAllAccess = async (person: PeopleRow, grant: boolean) => {\\r\\n    const who = person.display_name || person.email;\\r\\n    if (!grant && !window.confirm(\\r\\n      `Revoke all access for ${who}?\\r\\n\\r\\n` +\\r\\n      \'They keep their login. Everything else, including assigned work, \' +\\r\\n      \'notes, drill, bootcamps, review and messages, is locked until you grant it back.\',\\r\\n    )) return;\\r\\n\\r\\n    const flags: Record<string, boolean> = {};\\r\\n    for (const key of ALL_FLAG_KEYS) flags[key] = grant;\\r\\n\\r\\n    const previous = users;\\r\\n    setUsers((prev) => prev.map((user) => (user.class_id === person.class_id ? { ...user, ...flags } : user)));\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      const res = await supabase.functions.invoke(\'admin-manage-users\', {\\r\\n        method: \'POST\',\\r\\n        body: { action: \'apply_template\', class_id: person.class_id, flags },\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      if (res.error) throw res.error;\\r\\n      toast.success(grant ? `${who} has full access` : `${who} has no access`);\\r\\n"}}'}]

Content is static JSON; only\\r\\n                      progress touches the database. This route survives as a\\r\\n                      query-preserving adapter — see RcRedirect. */}\\r\\n                  <Route path=\\"/rc\\" element={\\r\\n                    <DrillAssignmentGate><RcRedirect /></DrillAssignmentGate>\\r\\n                  } />\\r\\n                  {/* The directions screen before an assigned section. Outside\\r\\n                      the Classroom shell on purpose: it takes the whole screen,\\r\\n                      like the section it leads into. */}\\r\\n                  <Route path=\\"/section/:assignmentId\\" element={\\r\\n                    <ProtectedRoute flag=\\"has_classroom_access\\"><SectionDirectionsPage /></ProtectedRoute>\\r\\n                  } />\\r\\n                  <Route path=\\"/rc/drill\\" element={\\r\\n                    <DrillAssignmentGate><RcPlayer /></DrillAssignmentGate>\\r\\n                  } />\\r\\n                  <Route path=\\"/rc/results/:sessionId\\" element={\\r\\n                    <ProtectedRoute allowSignedIn><RcResults /></ProtectedRoute>\\r\\n                  } />\\r\\n                  <Route path=\\"/rc/search\\" element={\\r\\n                    <ProtectedRoute flag=\\"has_drill_access\\"><RcSearch /></ProtectedRoute>\\r\\n                  } />\\r\\n                  {/* Legacy path — the Wrong Answer Journal is now the Wrong Answers section of the Journal. */}\\r\\n                  <Route path=\\"/waj\\" element={<Navigate to=\\"/journal\\" replace />} />\\r\\n                  {/* Dev-only harness for the Activity Centre worlds. The runner\\r\\n                      is behind auth + a feature flag, which makes a world\\r\\n                      impossible to look at while building it; this renders one\\r\\n                      directly against the real seeded content. Never registered\\r\\n                      in a production bundle. */}\\r\\n                  {import.meta.env.DEV && (\\r\\n                    <Route path=\\"/activity-lab\\" element={\\r\\n                      <React.Suspense fallback={<div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background\\" />}>\\r\\n                        <ActivityLab />\\r\\n                      </React.Suspense>\\r\\n                    } />\\r\\n                  )}\\r\\n                  {import.meta.env.DEV && (\\r\\n                    <Route path=\\"/conditional-lab\\" element={\\r\\n                      <React.Suspense fallback={<div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background\\" />}>\\r\\n                        <ConditionalPracticeLab />\\r\\n                      </React.Suspense>\\r\\n                    } />\\r\\n                  )}\\r\\n                  {/* Teacher preview. Runs off the activity DEFINITION and\\r\\n                      writes nothing, so looking at an activity never puts it on\\r\\n                      the teacher\'s own \\"Assigned to you\\" list. Admin only. */}\\r\\n                  <Route path=\\"/activities/preview/:activityId\\" element={\\r\\n                    <ProtectedRoute requireAdmin>\\r\\n                      <React.Suspense fallback={<div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background\\" />}>\\r\\n                        <ActivityRunner preview />\\r\\n                      </React.Suspense>\\r\\n                    </ProtectedRoute>\\r\\n                  } />\\r\\n                  {/* The Read, Recall, Colour door at a permanent address, so\\r\\n                      a student can pick a section without having been assigned\\r\\n                      the library. Must sit before :assignmentId. */}\\r\\n                  <Route path=\\"/activities/sections\\" element={\\r\\n                    <ProtectedRoute flag=\\"has_activity_access\\">\\r\\n                      <React.Suspense fallback={<div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background\\" />}>\\r\\n                        <ActivitySections />\\r\\n                      </React.Suspense>\\r\\n                    </ProtectedRoute>\\r\\n                  } />\\r\\n                  <Route path=\\"/activities/:assignmentId\\" element={\\r\\n                    <ActivityAssignmentGate>\\r\\n                      <React.Suspense fallback={<div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background\\" />}>\\r\\n                        <ActivityRunner />\\r\\n                      </React.Suspense>\\r\\n                    </ActivityAssignmentGate>\\r\\n                  } />\\r\\n                  <Route path=\\"/activities/conditionals\\" element={\\r\\n                    <ProtectedRoute flag=\\"has_activity_access\\">\\r\\n                      <React.Suspense fallback={<div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background\\" />}>\\r\\n                        <ConditionalPractice />\\r\\n                      </React.Suspense>\\r\\n                    </ProtectedRoute>\\r\\n                  } />\\r\\n\\r\\n                  {/* The Classroom frame. Every route drawn by it hangs off\\r\\n                      THIS ONE pathless layout route, which is what keeps the\\r\\n                      rail mounted across navigations — two sibling routes each\\r\\n                      holding their own <ClassroomLayout /> would unmount and\\r\\n                      remount it, which is exactly the shell-swap this design\\r\\n                      exists to eliminate.\\r\\n\\r\\n                      Each branch keeps its own guard, so /flagged still needs\\r\\n                      has_flagged_access and nothing about who can reach what\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"ddae6c","wall_time_seconds":0.3163596,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":769,"output":"import { useState } from \'react\';\\r\\nimport { useQuery, useQueryClient } from \'@tanstack/react-query\';\\r\\nimport { Check, X } from \'lucide-react\';\\r\\nimport { toast } from \'sonner\';\\r\\nimport { ACCESS_TIERS, type AccessTier, type ManagedUser } from \'@/lib/adminUsers\';\\r\\nimport { listManagedUsers, setStudentTier } from \'@/lib/accessTierClient\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport { setStudentTier };\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const SIGNUP_QUEUE_KEY = [\'signup-queue\'] as const;\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Undecided accounts: no tier yet, not archived, not an admin. Newest first. */\\r\\nexport function signupQueueRows(users: ManagedUser[]): ManagedUser[] {\\r\\n  return users\\r\\n    .filter((u) => u.role !== \'admin\' && !u.archived_at && !u.access_tier)\\r\\n    .sort((a, b) => (b.created_at ?? \'\').localeCompare(a.created_at ?? \'\'));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Everyone waiting on a decision: new signups and anyone with no tier. Until\\r\\n * Joshua decides, they have the floor (Classroom, Messages, Schedule) and\\r\\n * nothing else. The checkmark picks a tier; the X puts them on Tier 3, which\\r\\n * with nothing assigned is the same floor, just decided.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Rendered on Today and on Students. Both read the same query, so deciding on\\r\\n * one empties the row on the other.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function SignupQueue({ onChanged }: { onChanged?: () => void }) {\\r\\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\\r\\n  const [picking, setPicking] = useState<string | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<string | null>(null);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data: rows = [], isLoading, isError } = useQuery<ManagedUser[]>({\\r\\n    queryKey: SIGNUP_QUEUE_KEY,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => signupQueueRows(await listManagedUsers()),\\r\\n    staleTime: 30_000,\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  const decide = async (person: ManagedUser, tier: AccessTier) => {\\r\\n    const who = person.display_name || person.email;\\r\\n    setBusy(person.class_id);\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      await setStudentTier(person.class_id, tier);\\r\\n      const label = ACCESS_TIERS.find((t) => t.id === tier)?.label ?? tier;\\r\\n      toast.success(`${who} is on ${label}`);\\r\\n      setPicking(null);\\r\\n      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: SIGNUP_QUEUE_KEY });\\r\\n      onChanged?.();\\r\\n    } catch (error) {\\r\\n      console.error(\'signup queue: set_tier failed\', { class_id: person.class_id, tier, error });\\r\\n      toast.error(`Could not set a tier for ${who}`);\\r\\n    } finally {\\r\\n      setBusy(null);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (isLoading) return null;\\r\\n  if (isError) {\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <div className=\\"border border-black/12 bg-white/25 px-4 py-3.5 text-[13px] text-black/50\\">\\r\\n        The signup queue could not load.\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (rows.length === 0) return null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <section aria-label=\\"Signup queue\\" className=\\"border border-black/12 bg-white/25 px-4 py-3.5\\">\\r\\n      <div className=\\"mb-2.5 flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-black/40\\">\\r\\n          Waiting for a tier\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"text-[11px] tabular-nums text-black/40\\">{rows.length}</div>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n      <ul className=\\"divide-y divide-black/10\\">\\r\\n"}'}]

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-tier-defaults/.impeccable/live/tier-defaults.html\n+<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset=\"UTF-8\"><meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\"><title>Tier defaults local QA</title></head><body><div id=\"root\"></div><script type=\"module\" src=\"./tier-defaults.tsx\"></script></body></html>\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-tier-defaults/.impeccable/live/tier-defaults.tsx\n+import React, { useState } from 'react';\n+import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client';\n+import { AccessTierControl } from '../../src/pages/teacher/desk/DeskPeopleSurface';\n+import { tierFlags, withDerivedAccess, type AccessTier, type ManagedUser } from '../../src/lib/adminUsers';\n+import '../../src/index.css';\n+\n+function Fixture() {\n+  const [person, setPerson] = useState<ManagedUser>({ class_id: 'local-fixture', display_name: 'Local test student', email: 'fixture@example.test', role: 'user', last_seen_at: null, archived_at: null, archived_reason: null, access_tier: 'tier_1', ...tierFlags('tier_1') } as ManagedUser);\n+  const pick = (_person: ManagedUser, tier: AccessTier) => setPerson((row) => ({ ...row, ...tierFlags(tier), access_tier: tier }));\n+  return <main className=\"mx-auto max-w-4xl p-6\"><h1 className=\"mb-6 text-2xl\">Local tier controls</h1><AccessTierControl person={person} setTier={pick} toggleAccess={(_id, field, value) => setPerson((row) => ({ ...row, ...withDerivedAccess({ ...row, [field]: value } as unknown as Record<string, boolean>), access_tier: 'tier_2' }))} /><pre className=\"mt-6 overflow-auto text-xs\" data-testid=\"local-access-state\">{JSON.stringify(person, null, 2)}</pre></main>;\n+}\n+if (location.hostname !== '127.0.0.1' && location.hostname !== 'localhost') throw new Error('Local QA only');\n+createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(<Fixture />);\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-tier-defaults/docs/verification/student-tier-defaults.sql\n+-- Run AFTER the forward migration. Exercises only a temporary table, then rolls back.\n+BEGIN;\n+CREATE TEMP TABLE tier_policy_probe AS SELECT * FROM public.profiles WITH NO DATA;\n+CREATE TRIGGER tier_policy_probe_drift BEFORE UPDATE ON tier_policy_probe\n+FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.profiles_access_tier_drift();\n+\n+INSERT INTO tier_policy_probe (\n+  class_id, access_tier, has_drill_access, has_practice_access, has_classroom_access,\n+  has_chat_access, has_schedule_access, has_journal_access, has_flagged_access,\n+  has_analytics_access, has_activity_access, has_bootcamp_access, has_ta_access,\n+  has_export_access, has_waj_access\n+) VALUES ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001', NULL,\n+  false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false);\n+\n+DO $test$\n+DECLARE r record;\n+BEGIN\n+  -- Old Tier 2 endpoint changes only the label; the trigger supplies the preset.\n+  UPDATE tier_policy_probe SET access_tier = 'tier_2' RETURNING * INTO r;\n+  IF NOT (r.has_classroom_access AND r.has_chat_access AND r.has_schedule_access) OR\n+     r.has_drill_access OR r.has_practice_access OR r.has_analytics_access THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Tier 2 preset failed';\n+  END IF;\n+  UPDATE tier_policy_probe SET access_tier = 'tier_1' RETURNING * INTO r;\n+  IF NOT (r.has_drill_access AND r.has_practice_access AND r.has_classroom_access AND\n+    r.has_chat_access AND r.has_schedule_access AND r.has_journal_access AND\n+    r.has_flagged_access AND r.has_analytics_access) OR r.has_activity_access OR r.has_bootcamp_access THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Tier 1 preset failed';\n+  END IF;\n+  UPDATE tier_policy_probe SET has_drill_access = false RETURNING * INTO r;\n+  IF r.access_tier <> 'tier_2' OR NOT r.has_journal_access OR NOT r.has_analytics_access THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Tier 1 deviation or OR dependency failed';\n+  END IF;\n+  UPDATE tier_policy_probe SET has_practice_access = false RETURNING * INTO r;\n+  IF r.has_journal_access OR r.has_flagged_access OR r.has_analytics_access THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Review was not disabled with both practice doors off';\n+  END IF;\n+  UPDATE tier_policy_probe SET access_tier = 'tier_3' RETURNING * INTO r;\n+  IF r.has_classroom_access OR r.has_drill_access OR r.has_practice_access OR\n+     NOT r.has_chat_access OR NOT r.has_schedule_access THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Tier 3 preset failed';\n+  END IF;\n+  UPDATE tier_policy_probe SET has_classroom_access = true RETURNING * INTO r;\n+  IF r.access_tier <> 'tier_2' OR r.has_drill_access THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Tier 3 deviation failed';\n+  END IF;\n+  UPDATE tier_policy_probe SET has_activity_access = true, has_bootcamp_access = true RETURNING * INTO r;\n+  IF r.has_activity_access OR r.has_bootcamp_access THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Retired category grants survived';\n+  END IF;\n+  UPDATE tier_policy_probe SET archived_at = now(), has_chat_access = false,\n+    has_schedule_access = false, has_classroom_access = false RETURNING * INTO r;\n+  UPDATE tier_policy_probe SET archived_at = NULL RETURNING * INTO r;\n+  IF r.has_chat_access OR r.has_schedule_access OR r.has_classroom_access OR r.access_tier <> 'tier_2' THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unarchive reopened access or forgot tier';\n+  END IF;\n+END;\n+$test$;\n+ROLLBACK;\n*** End Patch"));

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.4 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"9fc300","wall_time_seconds":0.2437071,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":596,"output":"import type { TimerMode } from \'@/types/drill\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The page a student sees between \\"Open the set\\" and the first question of an\\r\\n * assigned PrepTest section: section number, the clock, the question count,\\r\\n * and the official directions. Modelled on LawHub\'s directions screen, because\\r\\n * the point of assigning a real section is that it feels like the real thing.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type SectionKind = \'lr\' | \'rc\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function sectionDirectionsRoute(assignmentId: string): string {\\r\\n  return `/section/${encodeURIComponent(assignmentId)}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** LSAC\'s own wording, verbatim. Not to be edited for voice. */\\r\\nexport const SECTION_DIRECTIONS: Record<SectionKind, string> = {\\r\\n  lr:\\r\\n    \'Each question in this section is based on the reasoning presented in a brief passage. \' +\\r\\n    \'In answering the questions, you should not make assumptions that are by commonsense \' +\\r\\n    \'standards implausible, superfluous, or incompatible with the passage. For some questions, \' +\\r\\n    \'more than one of the choices could conceivably answer the question. However, you are to \' +\\r\\n    \'choose the best answer; that is, choose the response that most accurately and completely \' +\\r\\n    \'answers the question.\',\\r\\n  rc:\\r\\n    \'Each set of questions in this section is based on a single passage. The questions are to \' +\\r\\n    \'be answered on the basis of what is stated or implied in the passage. For some questions, \' +\\r\\n    \'more than one of the choices could conceivably answer the question. However, you are to \' +\\r\\n    \'choose the best answer; that is, choose the response that most accurately and completely \' +\\r\\n    \'answers the question.\',\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Minutes on the clock for a timing mode, or null when there is no clock.\\r\\n * Standard is 35; 52.5 and 70 are the 1.5x and 2x accommodations.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function sectionMinutes(mode: TimerMode | undefined): number | null {\\r\\n  switch (mode) {\\r\\n    case \'52.5\':\\r\\n      return 52.5;\\r\\n    case \'70\':\\r\\n      return 70;\\r\\n    case \'unlimited\':\\r\\n      return null;\\r\\n    default:\\r\\n      return 35;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** \\"35 minutes\\", \\"52 minutes 30 seconds\\", \\"70 minutes\\". */\\r\\nexport function formatSectionMinutes(minutes: number): string {\\r\\n  const whole = Math.floor(minutes);\\r\\n  const seconds = Math.round((minutes - whole) * 60);\\r\\n  if (seconds === 0) return `${whole} minutes`;\\r\\n  return `${whole} minutes ${seconds} seconds`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

[];\\r\\n  const ordered = fromAssignment.length > 0 ? fromAssignment : fromDrill;\\r\\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\\r\\n  const out: string[] = [];\\r\\n  for (const qid of ordered) {\\r\\n    if (!qid || seen.has(qid)) continue;\\r\\n    if (knownQids.size > 0 && !knownQids.has(qid)) continue;\\r\\n    seen.add(qid);\\r\\n    out.push(qid);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return out;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function listGuidedDrills(): Promise<GuidedDrill[]> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\'guided_drills\')\\r\\n    .select(\'*\')\\r\\n    .order(\'week\', { ascending: true })\\r\\n    .order(\'order_in_week\', { ascending: true });\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  return (data || []) as GuidedDrill[];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function getGuidedDrill(id: string): Promise<GuidedDrill | null> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\'guided_drills\')\\r\\n    .select(\'*\')\\r\\n    .eq(\'id\', id)\\r\\n    .maybeSingle();\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  return (data as GuidedDrill) || null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function getGuidedDrillBySlug(slug: string): Promise<GuidedDrill | null> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\'guided_drills\')\\r\\n    .select(\'*\')\\r\\n    .eq(\'slug\', slug)\\r\\n    .maybeSingle();\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  return (data as GuidedDrill) || null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Resolve the question pool for a guided drill from the in-memory question bank.\\r\\n * Filters by qtypes (any) ∩ features (any) ∩ reasoning_types (any) ∩ difficulty range.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function resolveGuidedDrillPool(filter: GuidedDrillBankFilter | null | undefined) {\\r\\n  const all = questionBank.getAllQuestions();\\r\\n  const f = filter ?? {};\\r\\n  const qtypeSet = new Set((f.qtypes || []).map((s) => s.toLowerCase()));\\r\\n  const featSet = new Set((f.features || []).map((s) => s.toLowerCase()));\\r\\n  const rtSet = new Set((f.reasoning_types || []).map((s) => s.toLowerCase()));\\r\\n  const dmin = f.difficulty_min ?? null;\\r\\n  const dmax = f.difficulty_max ?? null;\\r\\n  return all.filter((q) => {\\r\\n    if (qtypeSet.size > 0 && !qtypeSet.has((q.qtype || \'\').toLowerCase())) return false;\\r\\n    if (featSet.size > 0) {\\r\\n      const hit = (q.questionFeatures || []).some((f) => featSet.has(f.toLowerCase()));\\r\\n      if (!hit) return false;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    if (rtSet.size > 0) {\\r\\n      const hit = (q.reasoningType || []).some((r) => rtSet.has(r.toLowerCase()));\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

Implementation finished and edits frozen in `lsatu-tier-defaults`. No commits, pushes, or backend deployment performed.

- Five visible controls; all three presets apply complete defaults, including reselecting the same tier.
- Drill OR Practice tests derives Wrong answers, Flagged questions, and Progress/history.
- Activities, Bootcamps, TA and Export controls removed.
- Assignment bypass requires Classroom; Tier 3 cannot reuse old assignment links.
- Homework carried questions constrained to assigned snapshot; guided identity/questions checked; bootcamp runners require the matching Intro-to-LR assignment.
- Authorization cache now includes launch scope.
- Saved-work review routes authorize owned records; PT-only includes RC review.
- Individual overrides write only the changed flag, avoiding concurrent-toggle resets.
- Forward migration and temporary-table rollback verification SQL prepared, with no bulk student updates.
- **54 focused tests passed across six files.**

Local actual-controls fixture: `http://127.0.0.1:8093/.impeccable/live/tier-defaults.html`. Parent already verified presets, derived controls and responsive widths. I did not add an authenticated assignment browser fixture; routed component tests cover ownership and Tier 3 denial.

Remaining parent work: final typecheck/build/lint, reviewed SQL application, Git release and production verification. Exclude unrelated generated `supabase/functions/mcp/index.ts` churn.

## assistant

Migration now sets the Classroom default to false for new profiles, without updating existing accounts.

Prepared `reset_onboarding` backend code keeps only Messages and Schedule enabled. Its deployed older version explicitly enables Classroom, so reset behavior requires deploying the updated function. Tier selection remains compatible through the reviewed database trigger and frontend fallback.

`handle_new_user` is absent from tracked migration sources. Its live definition must be checked for explicit Classroom grants before confirming new-signup behavior. No frontend changes made.

(\\r\\n          <aside className=\\"classroom-section-list\\">\\r\\n            <SectionList section={activeSection} activeId={activeId} />\\r\\n          </aside>\\r\\n        ) : null}\\r\\n\\r\\n        {journalDetail ? (\\r\\n          <React.Suspense fallback={<StageFallback />}>\\r\\n            <Outlet context={contextValue} />\\r\\n          </React.Suspense>\\r\\n        ) : messagesStage ? (\\r\\n          <main className=\\"classroom-messages-stage\\">\\r\\n            <React.Suspense fallback={<StageFallback />}>\\r\\n              <Outlet context={contextValue} />\\r\\n            </React.Suspense>\\r\\n          </main>\\r\\n        ) : workspaceStage ? (\\r\\n          <main className=\\"classroom-workspace-stage\\">\\r\\n            <React.Suspense fallback={<StageFallback />}>\\r\\n              <Outlet context={contextValue} />\\r\\n            </React.Suspense>\\r\\n          </main>\\r\\n        ) : (\\r\\n          <main className=\\"classroom-stage\\">\\r\\n            <div className=\\"classroom-column\\">\\r\\n              <React.Suspense fallback={<StageFallback />}>\\r\\n                <Outlet context={contextValue} />\\r\\n              </React.Suspense>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n          </main>\\r\\n        )}\\r\\n\\r\\n        {activeSection && sectionOverlayOpen ? \\"@/features/activity-centre/api/types\\";\\r\\nimport { localDayISO } from \\"@/lib/localDate\\";\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  pickContinueWork,\\r\\n  homeBookingFor,\\r\\n  KIND_LABEL,\\r\\n { manualCompletionAllowed, workSummary } from \\"./homeNextAction\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nconst RECAP_WINDOW_DAYS = 21;\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The candidate carries the timestamp a clear is recorded against. */\\r\\ntype MessageCandidate = HomeMessage & { lastMessageAt: string };\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Feed kinds map onto the model\'s kinds one-for-one. */\\r\\nconst FEED_KIND: Record<AssignedTodoRow[\\"kind\\"], HomeKind> = {\\r\\n  activity: \\"activity\\",\\r\\n  guided: \\"guided\\",\\r\\n  homework: \\"homework\\",\\r\\n  ta: \\"ta\\",\\r\\n  bootcamp: \\"bootcamp\\",\\r\\n  plan: \\"plan\\",\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction timeLabel(iso: string) {\\r\\n  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(\\"en-CA\\", {\\r\\n    hour: \\"numeric\\",\\r\\n    minute: \\"2-digit\\",\\r\\n  }).format(new Date(iso));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function useClassroomHome(): {\\r\\n  model: ClassroomHomeModel;\\r\\n  loading: boolean;\\r\\n  /** Close out an assigned row by key; no-op for a row that is not [\\r\\n    { key: \'guided\', refId: \'issued\', kind: \'guided\', title: \'Reasoning drill\', sourceTable: \'guided_drill_assignments\', subtitle: \'5 questions\', createdAt: \'2026-09-30T00:00:00Z\', dueAt: null, route: \'/guided/issued\', status: \'assigned\', progressUnverified: true },\\r\\n  ] : [], loading: false, failed: workState.uncertain, evidenceWarnings: workState.uncertain ? [\'saved guided sittings\'] : [], retry: vi.fn() }),\\r\\n  routeWithReturnTo: (route: string) => route,\\r\\n}));\\r\\nvi.mock(\\"@/hooks/useClassroomItems\\", () => ({\\r\\n  useClassroomItems: () home_cleared_at: string | null;\\r\\n  created_at: string;\\r\\n};\\r\\nlet thread: ThreadState;\\r\\nconst clearConversationFromHome = vi.fn(async () => unread: thread.unread,\\r\\n        home_cleared_at: thread.home_cleared_at,\\r\\n        last_message: {\\r\\n          sender_id: \\"joshua-id\\",\\r\\n          created_at: thread.created_at,\\r\\n          body: \\"How did the drill go?\\",\\r\\n        },\\r\\n        participants: [\\r\\n          { user_id: \\"joshua-id\\", display_name: \\"Joshua\\" },\\r\\n          { user_id: USER_ID, display_name: \\"Ada\\" },\\r\\n        ],\\r\\n      },\\r\\n    ],\\r\\n  }),\\r\\n}));\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { useClassroomHome } from \\"./useClassroomHome\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction wrapper({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {\\r\\n  const client = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <QueryClientProvider client={client}>\\r\\n - Classroom shell suppresses work/notes lists, mobile triggers and overlays when Classroom is off or loading.
